Existing benchmarks evaluate only one axis at a time and none jointly captures multi-identity composition with human-object interaction, background grounding, and spatial plausibility. We introduce CogCanvas, a benchmark of 1,952 curated reference images spanning 100 celebrity identities, 115 distinctive objects and fashion items, and 29 real-world background scenes including landmarks, from which we construct 1,361 compositional prompts covering 2-5 person group sizes. The curation pipeline combines DINOv2-based deduplication, two-stage aesthetic filtering, and automated derivation of structured interaction and position graphs that serve as ground-truth supervision. CogCanvas supports three tasks, reference-based multi-human-object generation (primary), text-to-image compositional generation, and reference retrieval, under a unified six-axis evaluation protocol. We introduce two metrics tailored to the multi-reference setting: BG-Sim, which scores background fidelity on SAM 3-masked regions via DINOv3 feature similarity, and Attr-VQA, which uses a multimodal LLM to verify per-subject attribute binding and inter-person interactions against the structured graphs. Benchmarking five SOTA methods reveals that every model degrades substantially as group size grows from 2 to 5, with near-complete failure on object/fashion binding beyond three subjects.

</details>


### [145] [Metis: A Generalizable and Efficient World-Action Model for Autonomous Driving and Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15869)
*Jingyu Li,Zhe Liu,Dongnan Hu,Junjie Wu,Zipei Ma,Wenxiao Wu,Chao Han,Zhihui Hao,Zhikang Liu,Kun Zhan,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Metis提出了一种解耦视频生成与动作预测的端到端世界动作模型框架，采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构和不对称注意力掩码，在保持训练-推理一致性的同时大幅降低推理延迟，在NAVSIM、CityWalker导航基准和真实机器人部署上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 测试时需预测未来观测导致高推理延迟；(2) 视频与动作紧密耦合导致表征不匹配和泛化能力下降。作者旨在解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出Metis框架：采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，包含视频生成和动作预测的专用专家模块；设计不对称注意力掩码，使得两个专家可联合训练，同时动作模型在推理时绕过显式视频生成，实现高效推理。

Result: 在NAVSIM的navhard/navtest基准和CityWalker导航基准上取得SOTA性能；通过真实机器人部署验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成与动作预测的WAM框架能有效提升效率与泛化能力，不对称注意力掩码的设计实现了训练-推理一致性，在不牺牲规划性能的前提下显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: World action models~(WAMs) have shown great promise for autonomous driving and urban navigation. Built upon Vision-Language-Action models or video generation models, existing approaches suffer key limitations: (1) High inference latency due to future observation prediction at test time, and (2) tightly coupled video and action modeling leading to representational mismatch and degraded generalization. To address both issues, we propose Metis, an end-to-end WAM framework that decouples video generation and action prediction. Specifically, Metis employs a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture with dedicated experts for video generation and action prediction, preserving the intrinsic distributional properties of each task. To enhance efficiency, we introduce an asymmetric attention mask that enables joint training of both experts while allowing the action model to bypass explicit video generation during inference. This design ensures training-inference consistency and significantly reduces computational costs without compromising planning performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the NAVSIM navhard and navtest benchmarks and the CityWalker navigation benchmark, validating both the generalizability and efficiency across diverse tasks. Real-robot deployments further confirm the practical feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [146] [Deep Residual Injection for Full-Spectrum Forensic Signal Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15880)
*Kaiqing Lin,Zhiyuan Yan,Ruoxin Chen,Ke-Yue Zhang,Yue Zhou,Caiyong Piao,Bin Li,Taiping Yao,Bo Wang,Youchang Xiao,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Deep-VRM，通过残差路径将低层伪造 artifacts 注入MLLM中间层，在保留高层语义能力的同时实现全频谱取证信号感知，达到SOTA检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在伪造检测中仅依赖语义级不一致，无法捕捉低层生成器伪影；直接微调会破坏预训练语义表示，因此需要一种既能保留语义知识又能学习底层artifact的检测框架。

Method: 提出Deep-VRM，通过分层分析发现语义信息在早期到中间层形成，设计残差路径将逐层提取的artifact视觉信号注入到中间层，与语义token表示融合后经后续可训练层传播，实现联合建模。

Result: 在多个benchmark上达到SOTA检测性能，模型能自适应地根据不同输入利用不同层次的取证信号，具备鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过残差注入方式在MLLM中实现全频谱取证信号感知是可行且有效的，为深度伪造检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted in forensics for their robust semantic understanding. As AI-generated images become realistic, semantic-level inconsistencies alone are often insufficient for reliable detection. This motivates a critical question: whether MLLMs can achieve full-spectrum forensic signal perception, i.e., capturing low-level generator artifacts without sacrificing pre-trained semantic knowledge. We further perform a layer-wise analysis of forensic signal perception in MLLMs, showing that semantic information is primarily formed in the early-to-middle layers, whereas direct fine-tuning for artifact learning disrupts these semantic representations. Based on this insight, we propose Deep Visual Residual MLLM (Deep-VRM) to preserve early semantic processing while injecting artifact-specific visual signals as a residual path into an intermediate layer, where they are fused with semantic token representations and propagated through subsequent trainable layers. This enables later layers to jointly model semantic reasoning and signal-level forensic cues, and surprisingly, the model learns to adaptively leverage different levels of forensic signals depending on the input, achieving robust and generalizable detection performance. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art across most benchmarks. The code and data are available at https://github.com/KQL11/Deep-VRM.

</details>


### [147] [Text region detection in historical astronomical diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15886)
*Zeynep Sonat Baltacı,Raphaël Baena,Fei Meng,Somkéo Norindr,Florence Somer,Matthieu Husson,Mathieu Aubry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个包含948张历史天文图表、10940个定向多边形文本区域的大规模数据集，涵盖10个世纪和7种主要语言传统，并设计了Poly-DETR方法在该任务上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文档中的文本检测任务已有研究，但数学图表（特别是天文图表）中的文本检测几乎未被关注，缺乏相关数据集和基准。

Method: 构建了涵盖10世纪7种语言传统的大规模天文图表文本检测数据集，以有序多边形标注文本区域；提出了Poly-DETR，在DINO-DETR基础上扩展以预测有序多边形顶点。

Result: Poly-DETR在MTHv2和cBAD2019基准上达到SOTA，并在天文图表数据集上提供了强基线。数据集和代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了历史天文图表文本检测的空白，Poly-DETR作为简单有效的基线方法，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Text detection is a crucial task in the analysis of historical documents. While datasets and benchmarks exist for text detection in manuscripts and maps, the study of text in mathematical diagrams has received little attention. To address this, we introduce a large-scale, diverse, open-access dataset of 948 historical astronomical diagrams containing 10,940 oriented polygonal text regions. Our dataset spans ten centuries (8th to 18th) and seven main linguistic traditions: Arabic and Persian (115), Chinese (332), Byzantine (233), Latin (185), Hebrew (48), and Sanskrit (35). It captures a wide range of diagram styles and textual content, from symbols to multi-line paragraphs. Each text instance is annotated with ordered polygons that precisely delineate text regions and encode the reading direction. In addition, we annotated the 2,293 regions in Latin diagrams with 20 class labels. We evaluated several strong baselines on our dataset, including TESTR, DeepSolo++, and Poly-DETR, a simple extension of DINO-DETR that we design to predict ordered polygon vertices. Poly-DETR achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MTHv2 and cBAD2019 benchmarks and provides a solid, simple baseline on our dataset. Code and dataset available online.

</details>


### [148] [SiGnature: Explicit Motion Diffusion for Stylized Semantic Gesture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15889)
*Adi Rosenthal,Tomer Koren,Nadav Shaked,Doron Friedman,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SiGnature框架，通过在显式关节旋转空间中操作并采用训练无关的Joint Motion Integration (JMI)机制，实现了在保留说话人风格的同时注入语义手势（如指向、示意动作）的共语手势生成，无需重训且避免拼接伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语手势生成方法虽能实现节奏同步，但难以同时生成语义有意义且忠实于说话人独特非语言风格的手势；语义手势（如指示性指向）在数据中统计稀疏，标准生成模型难以有效学习。

Method: 提出SiGnature框架，核心贡献为Joint Motion Integration (JMI)，一种训练无关的推理机制。模型在显式关节旋转空间（而非隐式潜在空间）中操作；JMI自动识别语义动作涉及的"活跃关节"，将其直接注入扩散过程的生成中，而由扩散骨干合成其余身体动态（姿态、流动），确保与目标说话人预学习风格一致，实现即插即用的外部动作注入。

Result: 大量实验和感知研究表明，SiGnature在语义运动控制上优于现有方法，同时生成流畅自然的共语手势并保留说话人的独特风格特征。

Conclusion: SiGnature通过在显式关节空间中进行训练无关的运动注入，成功解决了语义手势稀疏性和风格保留之间的权衡，无需重训即可集成任意外部动作，避免了拼接伪影，在共语手势生成中达到新SOTA。

Abstract: While recent advances in co-speech gesture generation have achieved impressive rhythmic synchronization, synthesizing gestures that are both semantically meaningful and faithful to a speaker's unique non-verbal style remains an open challenge. Semantic gestures, such as iconic shapes or deictic pointing, are statistically sparse, making them difficult to learn effectively within standard generative models. We present SiGnature, a framework for Stylized and Semantic Gesture generation that reconciles precise semantic control with high-fidelity style preservation. Unlike prevalent methods that rely on entangled latent representations, SiGnature operates in an explicit joint-rotation space. This design enables our core contribution, Joint Motion Integration (JMI), a training-free inference mechanism capable of injecting any external motion sequence, particularly in-the-wild semantic gestures, directly into the diffusion process. JMI automatically identifies the specific ``active joints'' conveying a semantic action and injects them into the generation, while relying on the diffusion backbone to synthesize the remaining body dynamics, including posture and flow, in accordance with the pre-learned style of the target speaker. This allows for the plug-and-play integration of arbitrary motions, including complex semantic gestures, without retraining or introducing the ``Frankenstein'' artifacts typical of cut-and-paste methods. Extensive experiments and perceptual studies demonstrate that SiGnature offers superior semantic motion control while maintaining smooth and natural co-speech gesture generation and preserving the distinct characteristics of the speaker, thereby outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [149] [High-Fidelity 4D Hand-Object Capture via Multi-View Spatiotemporal Tracking and Physics-Aware Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15908)
*Bo Peng,Xu Chen,Yi Gu,Hidenobu Matsuki,Mingsong Dou,Jingjing Shen,Deying Kong,Juyong Zhang,Zhengyang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一套无需模板和标记的多视图4D手物交互重建系统HOSTPG，包含多视图前馈Transformer初始化 + 基于物理感知的高斯优化，显著提升遮挡下重建的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D手物交互数据重建严重依赖预扫描物体模板和物理标记，而基于视频的方法对手和物体初始位姿估计极为敏感，尤其在严重遮挡场景下不可靠。需要一套无需模板标记、鲁棒且准确的重建方法。

Method: 双组件系统：(1) 多视图前馈Transformer模型，聚合跨视角几何与时序线索，提供可靠的度量一致的初始化（位姿+稠密几何）；(2) 手物物理感知高斯优化框架，集成四面体约束、碰撞精修、外观分解，产生物理合理且视觉精确的重建。

Result: 在公开基准和内部大规模数据集上验证，实现了高度鲁棒、无伪影的重建，为自动4D资产生成提供高效基础。开源代码。

Conclusion: 无需模板和标记的多视图手物交互重建取得突破，物理感知优化显著提升遮挡场景下的几何和外观质量，可支撑4D资产自动化管线。

Abstract: The growing demand for high-fidelity 4D hand-object interaction (HOI) data in embodied AI and spatial computing is currently bottlenecked by the reliance on pre-scanned object templates and physical markers. While recent methods have demonstrated promising results in reconstructing 4D hand-object interaction from videos, they are highly sensitive to initial estimates of hand and object poses. Yet, estimating these poses from images is challenging, in particular under severe occlusion which is inherent in hand-object interaction scenarios. We propose a novel system for the robust and accurate reconstruction of hands and objects from synchronized and calibrated multi-view videos without requiring any templates or markers. Our system consists of two main components with key innovations: (1) a multi-view feed-forward transformer model that aggregates cross-view geometry and temporal cues to provide a reliable, metric-consistent initialization for both poses and dense object geometry, and (2) a hand-object physics-aware Gaussian-based optimization framework to refine the initial estimates, integrating tetrahedral constraints, collision refinement, and appearance decomposition to produce physically plausible and visually accurate reconstruction. Validated on public benchmarks and an extensive internal dataset, our pipeline achieves highly robust, artifact-free reconstruction, providing an efficient foundation for automated 4D asset generation. Our project page are available at https://zyshen021.github.io/HOSTPG/.

</details>


### [150] [OmniOPSD: Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Affective Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15920)
*Zebang Cheng,Shuimu Chen,Boxue Yang,Yuanshen Guan,Jingyi Chen,Zheng Lian,Xiaojiang Peng,Fei Ma,LaiZhong Cui,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniOPSD框架，利用前沿模型生成的推理依据作为教师侧的特权证据，在训练时提供密集token级监督，而学生仅从原始多模态输入采样自 rollouts，不直接模仿前沿模型输出，解决了MLLM在复杂推理任务中的奖励稀疏问题，在MER-UniBench上达到SOTA（84.19分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂推理任务（特别是人机交互场景）中面临严重的奖励稀疏问题，且高质量思维链标注成本高昂；直接使用标注标签进行监督微调容易导致多模态感知中的捷径学习，缺乏可解释性和安全性。

Method: 基于Rationale-Privileged的在线策略自蒸馏框架：前沿模型生成的推理依据仅作为训练时教师侧的特权证据上下文，教师对同一token提供密集级监督，学生从原始多模态输入中采样自身轨迹进行学习，不直接模仿前沿模型输出，推理时无需任何标签、推理依据或闭源模型。

Result: 在MER-UniBench上平均得分84.19，达到SOTA；消融实验验证了基于推理依据的教师指导的有效性。

Conclusion: OmniOPSD通过特权蒸馏范式的设计，在不依赖推理时额外标注或闭源模型的前提下，有效缓解了奖励稀疏问题，提升了多模态推理性能与训练透明度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is often hindered by severe reward sparsity in complex reasoning tasks. This challenge is particularly pronounced in human-centered scenarios involving states, emotions, intentions, and behaviors, where heterogeneous multimodal signals and subjective human factors make high-quality chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations expensive and difficult to obtain. Although many multimodal datasets provide expert-annotated ground-truth labels, directly using these labels for supervised fine-tuning may encourage shortcut learning in multimodal perception and provides limited transparency for safety-critical human--AI interaction. To address these limitations, we propose OmniOPSD, a Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation framework that uses frontier-generated rationales as teacher-side privileged evidence rather than student imitation targets. OmniOPSD uses frontier-generated evidence-aware rationales only as training-time privileged evidence context for a local teacher. The student samples its own rollout from the original multimodal input, while the rationale-privileged teacher scores the same tokens and provides dense token-level supervision. Thus, the student learns on its own trajectory distribution without directly imitating frontier-model completions, and inference requires no labels, rationales, CoT annotations, or closed-source model access. Experiments on MER-UniBench show that OmniOPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average score of $84.19$, and ablations further support the value of rationale-privileged teacher guidance.

</details>


### [151] [TurboGS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting via Error-Guided Sparse Pixel Sampling and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15924)
*Zheng Dong,Daifei Qiu,Pinxuan Dai,Ke Xu,Jiamin Xu,Lili He,Rynson W. H. Lau,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboGS是一种基于误差引导的3D高斯泼溅加速训练框架，通过稀疏像素采样、结构感知损失、误差驱动密度控制和混合优化器，在单个RTX 5090上100秒内实现与原始3DGS相当或更优的渲染质量，训练速度提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级应用需要快速优化3DGS以实现高保真新视图渲染，但现有加速方法在冗余像素上仍有大量计算且牺牲细节。

Method: (1) 基于多视图重建误差的瓦片级稀疏像素采样；(2) 瓦片级结构感知损失（稀疏NCC）；(3) 误差驱动的高斯密度控制策略；(4) Hessian信息与Adam动量阻尼耦合的混合优化器。

Result: 在标准基准上，单张RTX 5090上100秒内达成与原始3DGS相当或更优的渲染质量，最高10倍训练加速。

Conclusion: TurboGS通过误差引导的稀疏优化有效加速了3DGS训练，同时保持高保真渲染质量，适用于消费级应用。

Abstract: Consumer-level applications require fast optimization of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with high-fidelity novel view rendering. However, existing 3DGS acceleration approaches still incur substantial computation on redundant pixels while sacrificing fine details. In this paper, we present TurboGS, an error-guided training framework that accelerates 3DGS by concentrating optimization on perceptually informative pixels. TurboGS is built upon four core components: (1) a tile-wise sparse pixel sampling, which, driven by multi-view reconstruction errors during training, prioritizes challenging regions and skips well-reconstructed ones to avoid redundant gradient computation; (2) a tile-wise structure-aware loss with sparse Normalized Cross-Correlation, which provides sparse yet effective supervision to preserve fine details and stabilize training; (3) an error-driven Gaussian density control strategy, which dynamically allocates model capacity and removes redundant primitives; and (4) a tailored hybrid optimizer that couples Hessian-informed updates with Adam moment damping to stabilize and improve convergence under sparse supervision. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that TurboGS can deliver on par or superior rendering quality within 100 seconds on a single RTX 5090 GPU card (up to 10x training speedup over vanilla 3DGS).

</details>


### [152] [GOOSE-M2F: Adapting Mask2Former for High-Fidelity, Long-Tailed Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation in Unstructured Outdoor Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15937)
*Jyothiraditya Lingam,Nikhileswara Rao Sulake,Sai Manikanta Eswar Machara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOOSE-M2F是Mask2Former针对GOOSE细粒度语义分割挑战的改进版本，通过200个目标查询、特征精化模块（ASPP-lite+CBAM双注意力）、辅助监督头以及多阶段训练策略（分布平衡损失、稀有类复制粘贴增强、动态IoU重加权、EMA），在GOOSE 2D FGSS榜单上以70.08% Composite mIoU获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GOOSE基准包含64个细粒度类别的非结构化户外地形数据，存在严重的长尾分布，稀有类别每张图像少于50像素，现有Mask2Former对此场景表现不足。

Method: (1) 200个目标查询消除表征饱和；(2) 特征精化模块（ASPP-lite + CBAM双注意力）；(3) 辅助监督头提供逐像素梯度；(4) 多阶段训练：分布平衡损失、稀有类复制粘贴增强、动态IoU重加权、EMA；(5) 推理时密集滑动窗口引擎+2D高斯核融合+4尺度TTA。

Result: 70.08% Official Composite mIoU（63.55% fine, 76.61% coarse），GOOSE 2D FGSS榜单第三名。TTA带来+10.57%提升。

Conclusion: 通过多组件的针对性改进，证明在长尾细粒度语义分割任务上，增加查询数、双注意力特征精化、辅助监督头及多阶段训练策略能有效提升稀有类分割性能。

Abstract: We present GOOSE-M2F, a task-specific adaptation of Mask2Former for the GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation (FGSS) Challenge at ICRA~2026. The GOOSE benchmark spans 64 fine-grained classes across unstructured outdoor terrain with a severely long-tailed distribution, where rare classes occupy fewer than 50 pixels per image. We extend the Swin-Large Mask2Former baseline with three targeted contributions: (1)200 Object Queries to eliminate representational saturation; (2)a Feature Refinement Module (FRM) combining ASPP-lite and CBAM dual-attention; and (3)an Auxiliary Supervision Head that delivers direct per-pixel gradients for rare classes. A multi-stage training strategy pairs Distribution-Balanced loss, Rare-Class Copy-Paste augmentation, dynamic IoU-aware re-weighting, and EMA. At inference, a dense sliding-window engine with 2D Gaussian kernel blending and 4-scale TTA adds +10.57\%. GOOSE-M2F achieves 70.08\% Official Composite mIoU (63.55\% fine, 76.61\% coarse), placing 3rd on the GOOSE 2D FGSS leaderboard. Code and trained models are publicly available at: \href{https://github.com/Aditya-Lingam-9000/GOOSE-M2F}{Github GOOSE-M2F Code} and \href{https://huggingface.co/XYZ9843/GOOSE-M2F}{Hugging Face GOOSE-M2F}.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Directional Semantic Transitions for Longitudinal Chest X-ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15938)
*Zhangfeng Hu,Zefan Yang,Ge Wang,Tanveer Syeda-Mahmood,Anushree Burade,Mannudeep Kalra,Pingkun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProTrans是一个面向纵向胸部X光片分析的视觉-语言预训练框架，将疾病进展建模为成对CXR之间的方向性语义转换，通过放射报告锚定影像表征、可学习进展特征图编码语义偏移、反向时间建模与双向重建一致性约束来实现方向感知，在疾病进展分类和进展描述生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纵向CXR分析方法依赖时序特征融合或互研究差异建模，但难以捕获细微的进展语义，且忽视了疾病轨迹固有的方向性。需要一种能显式建模方向性语义演变的预训练框架。

Method: 1) 利用放射报告将CXR表征锚定到可解释疾病状态；2) 引入可学习进展特征图编码状态间的语义偏移，并与报告导出的进展描述对齐；3) 加入反向时间建模过程，施加状态和转变之间的双向重建一致性约束，以解耦方向语义。

Result: 在疾病进展分类和进展描述生成等纵向下游任务上，ProTrans一致优于现有方法，建立了统一的纵向CXR理解预训练框架。

Conclusion: ProTrans通过显式建模方向性语义转变，有效提升了纵向CXR分析能力，验证了方向感知预训练在医学影像时序理解中的价值。

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation often requires longitudinal comparison to assess disease progression. Existing approaches typically rely on temporal feature fusion or inter-study discrepancy modeling, yet remain limited in capturing subtle progression semantics and overlook the inherently directional nature of disease trajectories. In this paper, we propose ProTrans, a novel vision-language pretraining framework that formulates disease progression as a directional semantic transition between paired CXR studies. ProTrans leverages radiology reports to anchor individual CXR representations within interpretable disease states, and introduces a learnable progression feature map to explicitly encode semantic shifts between states, aligned with report-derived progression descriptions. To enforce direction-aware perception, ProTrans incorporates a reversed temporal modeling process and imposes bidirectional reconstruction consistency across states and transitions, thereby disentangling directional semantics and promoting coherent trajectory modeling. Extensive experiments on longitudinal downstream tasks, including disease progression classification and progression captioning, demonstrate that ProTrans consistently outperforms existing methods, establishing a unified pretraining framework for longitudinal CXR understanding. https://github.com/RPIDIAL/ProTrans

</details>


### [154] [You Don't Need Strong Assumptions: Visual Representation Learning via Temporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15956)
*Ninad Daithankar,Alexi Gladstone,Yann LeCun,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDV（时间差分视觉），一种从视频中自监督学习的全新范式，通过因果假设（过去导致未来）替代了现有自监督学习方法中的强归纳偏置（如数据增强、掩码、裁剪）。实验表明，随着数据规模增长，归纳偏置的最优强度会下降，而TDV在不依赖任何强归纳偏置的情况下，在密集空间任务上达到了SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究趋势显示，随着计算和数据增加，弱归纳偏置的方法优于强假设的方法。即使在自监督学习中，仍存在数据增强、掩码等强归纳偏置，这些偏置可能会成为大规模学习的瓶颈。论文通过实验验证了这一假设，并寻求消除这些偏置的新方法。

Method: 提出TDV框架，联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，使得当前帧的表示加上编码的运动等于下一帧的表示。核心是因果假设：过去导致未来，不使用数据增强、掩码、裁剪等强归纳偏置。

Result: 实验证实归纳偏置的最优强度随数据增长而下降；TDV在不使用任何强归纳偏置的情况下，在密集空间任务上匹配现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过因果假设替代强归纳偏置是可行的，TDV为无强假设的表示学习奠定了基础，未来有望在更大规模数据上发挥优势。

Abstract: Progress in AI has largely been driven by methods that assume less. As compute and data increase, approaches with weaker inductive biases generally outperform those with stronger assumptions. This is particularly characteristic of the field of Visual Representation Learning, where approaches have gone from being dominated by Supervised Learning, to Weakly Supervised Learning, to the now widespread success of Self-Supervised Learning without human labels. Yet, even modern Self-Supervised Learning approaches still depend on strong inductive biases such as augmentations, masking, or cropping. If this trend holds, even these remaining biases should become bottlenecks at scale -- and our experiments confirm this: the optimal strength of inductive biases decreases as data grows. This motivates the search for approaches that rely on fewer assumptions. To this end, we introduce Temporal Difference in Vision (TDV), a new paradigm for self-supervised learning from video that avoids existing inductive biases, relying instead on a causal assumption that the past causes the future. TDV functions by jointly training an image encoder and a motion encoder so that the current frame's representation plus the encoded motion equals the next frame's representation. Despite not leveraging any strong inductive biases, TDV matches state-of-the-art recipes on dense spatial tasks, laying the foundation for representation learning without strong assumptions.

</details>


### [155] [VEPHand: View-Efficient Photometric Hand Performance Capture at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15966)
*Zhengyang Shen,Kai-Hung Chang,Erroll Wood,Deying Kong,Bo Peng,Timo Bolkart,Jinlong Yang,Bowen Zhao,Danhang Tang,Sasa Petrovic,Emre Aksan,Jérémy Riviere,Vassilis Choutas,Delio Vicini,Jay Busch,Shichen Liu,Zhe Cao,Hugh Liu,JingJing Shen,Jonathan Taylor,Mingsong Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向少视角（约20视图）的动态手部性能捕捉与配准端到端流水线，核心创新包括：1）无掩码神经重建方法，利用场景参数化和密度正则化从无掩码图像中提取手部几何与外观；2）基于物理启发的配准框架，通过优化规范四面体网格内的固有体积偏移和姿态参数，实现非线性皮肤变形建模和严重自接触下的合理结果。在12000+序列上验证了可扩展性，并生成了大规模高质量合成手部数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少视角多视图系统中，手部动态捕捉面临几何模糊、视图重叠有限、背景杂乱、自接触复杂等挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾高保真重建和精确配准。

Method: 1) 无掩码神经重建：场景参数化+密度正则化从无掩码图像中提取手部几何和外观；2) 物理启发配准框架：在规范四面体网格中优化固有体积偏移和姿态参数，结合鲁棒损失函数，捕捉精细表面变形并保证自接触合理性。

Result: 在12000+序列数据集上验证了可扩展性和鲁棒性，在少视角无掩码场景下实现了SOTA重建保真度和高精度配准，适用于单手、双手交互和手物操作。

Conclusion: 方法在视图高效设置下达到了领先的重建和配准性能，生成的合成数据集可服务于下游任务。

Abstract: Robust, high-fidelity 3D hand capture, while fundamental to digital human creation, remains challenging with practical multi-view systems that balance rich photometry with the geometric ambiguities of reconstruction arising from limited viewpoint density. This paper presents an end-to-end pipeline for dynamic hand performance capture and registration, specifically designed for view-efficient setups ($\sim$20 views). We address key challenges with two primary innovations. First, to overcome reconstruction difficulties like limited view overlap and background clutter, our mask-free neural method robustly extracts detailed hand geometry and appearance from unmasked images using scene parameterization and scenario-specific density regularization. Second, addressing registration challenges such as accurately capturing non-linear skin deformations and ensuring plausible results during severe self-contact, we propose a physics-inspired framework. It aligns reconstructions to a personalized hand model by optimizing intrinsic volumetric offsets within its canonical tetrahedral mesh, alongside pose parameters. This approach, supported by robust losses and optimization, captures fine surface deformations, ensures plausible results under severe articulation and self-contact, and demonstrates strong tolerance to input noise. We demonstrate the scalability and robustness of our automated pipeline on an extensive dataset of over 12,000 sequences, from which we also derive a large-scale, high-quality synthetic 2D/3D hand dataset for training downstream tasks. This showcases its effectiveness for single hands, intricate two-hand interactions, and natural hand-object manipulations. Our method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity in view-efficient, unmasked scenarios and highly accurate registration. Our project page are available at https://zyshen021.github.io/VEPHand/.

</details>


### [156] [CRIS: Cross-Plane Self-Supervised Isotropic Restoration for Anisotropic Volumetric Imaging Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15967)
*Adi Ahituv,Anat Ilivitzki,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRIS框架，通过跨平面自监督学习实现各向异性3D数据（临床MRI和体积电镜）的各向同性恢复，无需配对各向同性真值。核心思想是将3D恢复建模为2D条带补全任务，在正交重切面上训练，推理时通过多视角融合生成各向同性体素。在MRI和vEM上显著优于插值及其他方法，且具有模态灵活性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床MRI和体积电镜中广泛存在各向异性采集（面内高分辨率、面间稀疏采样），导致正交重切面质量严重退化，影响下游分析。现有方法或需要配对各向同性真值（难以获取），或为特定配置定制化训练，缺乏通用且无需真值的解决方案。

Method: CRIS将3D各向同性恢复转化为2D条带补全任务：在正交重切面上，将高分辨率面内切片进行合成退化并周期性掩码为条带模式用于训练；推理时在目标各向同性网格的空白切片上，对两个正交重切面分别恢复，再通过多视图平均融合预测结果。

Result: 脑MRI: 32.921 dB PSNR, 0.9631 SSIM, 分割Dice 0.940; 无参考腹部MRI: FID 48.714; vEM: 4x各向异性下29.133 dB/0.834, 8x EPFL 27.123 dB/0.734; 鲁棒性实验: 单一模型在3-7倍各向异性及不同切面退化下均优于插值。

Conclusion: CRIS提供了一种模态灵活的各向同性恢复路径，无需配对目标数据或特定配置重训练，在MRI和vEM两个领域均取得领先性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Anisotropic volumetric acquisitions are common in clinical MRI and volume electron microscopy (vEM), where sparse through-plane sampling creates thick slices or sections that degrade orthogonal reformats and downstream analysis. We present CRIS, a cross-plane self-supervised framework for isotropic restoration without paired isotropic ground truth. CRIS casts 3D restoration as 2D stripe completion on orthogonal reformats of an isotropic grid: high-resolution in-plane slices are synthetically degraded and periodically masked for training, while at inference blank slices define the isotropic grid, two orthogonal reformats are restored, and predictions are fused by multi-view averaging. We evaluate CRIS on two MRI cohorts and two microscopy benchmarks up to 8x anisotropy. On brain MRI, CRIS achieves 32.921 +/- 0.436 dB PSNR and 0.9631 +/- 0.0027 SSIM, outperforming interpolation, SMORE4, SIMPLE, SA-INR, and ATME, and gives the best segmentation consistency (Dice 0.940 +/- 0.004, ASSD 0.245 +/- 0.014 mm, HD99 1.275 +/- 0.061 mm). On reference-free abdominal MRI, CRIS reduces FID/KID to 48.714/0.023. On vEM, CRIS outperforms interpolation, NIIV, and vEMINR, reaching 29.133 dB/0.834 3D PSNR/SSIM at 4x, 27.123 dB/0.734 on EPFL at 8x, and 21.915 dB/0.699 on noisy hemibrain data. In a robustness experiment, one variable-gap CRIS model evaluated across gap factors 3--7 and coronal, axial, and sagittal degradations maintained higher PSNR/SSIM than interpolation (36.36--31.14 dB and 0.977--0.932 vs. 33.07--27.85 dB and 0.951--0.853). These results support CRIS as a modality-flexible route to isotropic restoration without paired isotropic targets or configuration-specific retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/adi-hatav/CRIS.

</details>


### [157] [HadBalance: A Plug-and-Play Unified Global Geometric Prior Framework for Generalizable Biomedical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15976)
*Zhuangzhi Gao,Feixiang Zhou,He Zhao,Wenhan Chen,Ruiyu Luo,Xin Wang,Hongyi Qin,Zhongli Wu,Yanda Meng,Yitian Zhao,Alena Shantsila,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Eduard Shantsila,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HadBalance框架，基于Hadwiger定理推导形状先验（面积、周长、欧拉示性数）作为可解释全局正则化器用于医学图像分割，并设计冲突感知目标平衡（CAOB）机制处理形状异质数据，防止过度正则化破坏非凸解剖结构的细小特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何先验（边界、形状、拓扑）多为任务特定，缺乏统一几何基础，难以跨器官和模态泛化；同时医学分割目标多可近似为全局近凸形状，但数据集中存在形状异质，统一施加近凸先验会过度正则化非凸解剖结构，丢失真实凹陷和细节。

Method: 1) 从Hadwiger定理推导Hadwiger形状先验，使用面积A、周长P、欧拉示性数χ三个2D度量作为全局可解释正则化器；2) 提出冲突感知目标平衡（CAOB），对每个先验，以梯度感知方式只移除与分割目标冲突的梯度分量，保留对齐部分，并自适应调节各目标影响权重，防止先验主导。

Result: 在形状异质数据集上，HadBalance能在利用形状先验提升分割结构一致性的同时，不抹去真实凹陷和精细结构细节，避免过度正则化导致的精度下降。

Conclusion: HadBalance是一个即插即用框架，通过Hadwiger形状先验和CAOB机制，统一了跨器官/模态的几何正则化，有效解决了形状异质数据上的过度正则化问题。

Abstract: Precise biomedical image segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis. Geometric cues (e.g., boundary, shape, and topology) can improve structural consistency, yet most are task-specific and lack a unified geometric foundation that generalizes across organs and modalities. We are motivated by the observation that several medical segmentation targets can be approximated as globally near-convex shapes. A convex region is one in which any two interior points can be connected by a line segment entirely contained within the region. In practice, medical targets may exhibit small local concavities or boundary irregularities; we refer to such globally convex-like shapes as near-convex. Motivated by this, we derive Hadwiger Shape Priors from Hadwiger's theorem as an interpretable global regularizer using three 2D measures: area A, perimeter P, and Euler characteristic chi, enabling transfer across organs and modalities. However, because medical datasets are shape-heterogeneous, enforcing near-convex priors uniformly can over-regularize non-convex anatomy with significant concavities, washing out concavities and fine details and degrading segmentation accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Aware Objective Balancing (CAOB), which integrates shape priors with segmentation in a gradient-aware manner. For each prior, CAOB removes only the gradient component that conflicts with segmentation while preserving the remaining aligned component, and adaptively regulates objective influences to prevent prior dominance. This enables stable use of shape priors on shape-heterogeneous data without erasing genuine concavities or fine structural details. We call this plug-and-play framework HadBalance.

</details>


### [158] [Mind the Gap: Diagnosing Constraint Discovery Failures in Text-in-Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15982)
*Rui Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态推理中的一个关键挑战：模型如何自主发现任务相关的视觉依赖关系。通过文本编辑图像任务，构建461个诊断案例，发现MLLMs在无提示下仅恢复46%的约束（显式提供时为94%），失败主因是模型无法决定哪些隐性依赖需要被呈现。因果解释是最有效的部分引导（召回率0.782），但更高的自发现召回率并不一定提升任务性能——未经验证的自发现引入假阳性抵消收益，需要精度感知的约束发掘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理中，模型不仅要识别可见内容，还要确定哪些视觉依赖关系在特定任务下是相关的。已有研究缺乏对这一过程的受控诊断，本文通过文本编辑图像中的约束发现来系统研究这一问题。

Method: 构建461个诊断案例，涵盖19种约束子类型，使用4种MLLM在文本编辑图像任务中测试。采用无引导提示、显式约束、Oracle场分解（因果解释/区域名称/类型标签）等方法进行对比分析，并进行下游任务实验。

Result: 无引导下仅46%案例级宏召回率，显式约束时达94%。因果解释是最有效的部分引导（0.782召回率），优于区域名称（0.610）和类型标签（0.646）。更高的自发现召回率不一定提升任务性能，假阳性会抵消收益。

Conclusion: 模型在多模态推理中失败的主要瓶颈在于决定哪些未声明的依赖需要被呈现，而非识别能力本身。精度感知的约束发掘比单纯提高召回率更重要。

Abstract: A key challenge in multimodal reasoning is determining which visual dependencies become relevant under a specific task, rather than merely recognizing visible content. We study this through edit-induced constraint discovery in text-in-image editing, a controlled diagnostic setting where a local text change can activate secondary consistency constraints: given a valid editing instruction and an image, can a model identify the secondary regions that must also change? Across 461 diagnostic cases, four MLLMs, and 19 constraint subtypes, models recover only 46% case-level macro recall under unguided prompting versus 94% when constraints are explicitly provided, suggesting that a substantial portion of the failure arises when models must decide which unstated dependencies to surface. Oracle-field decomposition shows that case-specific causal explanations are the most effective partial guidance (0.782 recall), above region names (0.610) or type labels (0.646), suggesting that edit-specific causal cues account for much of the oracle gain. A downstream experiment further shows that higher self-discovery recall does not necessarily improve task performance: unverified self-discovery introduces false positives that offset recall gains, motivating precision-aware constraint elicitation.

</details>


### [159] [A Text Recognition Dataset from Sahidic Coptic Ancient Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15987)
*Fabio Quattrini,Carmine Zaccagnino,Costanza Bianchi,Silvia Cascianelli,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAM数据集——基于灭绝的萨希迪克科普特语古代手稿构建的行级手写文本识别(HTR)基准，聚焦低资源场景（稀有语言、罕见文字、历史文档退化），评估了多种SOTA方法，揭示了当前HTR在低资源场景与高资源现代文本之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源手写文本识别（HTR）面临语言资源稀缺、文字罕见和历史文档视觉退化等多重挑战，现有数据集和基准多聚焦于现代高资源语言，缺乏对极端低资源场景的系统研究。

Method: 构建SCAM数据集，收集多个图书馆数字化馆藏中的萨希迪克科普特语手稿，涵盖墨迹褪色、透印、材料退化等真实退化情况；采用多种范式（CNN/RNN/Transformer等）的SOTA方法进行基准测试。

Result: 揭示了当前HTR方法在低资源历史手稿与现代高资源文本之间存在显著性能差距，并识别了不同方法在处理罕见文字、方言变音符号和历史退化方面的优缺点。

Conclusion: SCAM为低资源HTR研究提供了具有挑战性的真实基准，指出了现有方法的不足，并为未来低资源场景下的HTR发展提供了参考点。

Abstract: In this work, we target Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) in low-resource scenarios, which arise from underrepresented languages, rare scripts, and degraded visual conditions typical of historical documents. We introduce SCAM (Sahidic Coptic Ancient Manuscripts), a new line-level dataset built from digitized ancient manuscripts written in the extinct Sahidic Coptic dialect. The dataset reflects a realistic and challenging setting, as it combines heterogeneous acquisition conditions across libraries with typical manuscript degradations such as ink fading, bleed-through, and material deterioration. In addition to visual complexity, SCAM poses significant linguistic challenges due to the scarcity of resources for Sahidic Coptic, its uncommon alphabet, and dialect-specific diacritics. To support research in low-resource HTR, we benchmark several state-of-the-art approaches based on different paradigms, highlighting their limitations and strengths in this setting. Our results underline the gap between current HTR performance on well-resourced modern scripts and historically grounded, low-resource scenarios, thus providing a reference point for future developments.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Task Tennis Stroke Biomechanics Analysis Using MediaPipe Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15992)
*Jigyashman Hazarika*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于RGB视频的多任务网球击球生物力学分析pipeline，包含击球类型识别、方向预测和姿势评分三个任务，使用自监督的加权关节速度分数自动检测击球，采用TennisTransformerGPU模型（56.4万参数），在跨球员评估中击球类型准确率保持稳定（82.9%），但方向预测退化为多数类，world coordinates至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从普通RGB视频中自动分析网球击球生物力学，减少人工标注需求，提供击球类型、方向和姿势质量等多维度评估，并生成可操作的教练反馈建议。

Method: 使用加权关节速度分数s(t)=0.5v_wrist+0.3m_elbow+0.2m_shoulder自动检测击球；MediaPipe提取33个3D landmarks；TennisTransformerGPU（4层4头d=128，564,103参数，三个并行输出头）处理30帧×39特征的序列；包含规则基础的反馈层。

Result: 训练集（7职业+1业余，11视频，1281个标注击球）80/20随机划分：击球类型83.7%、方向61.9%、姿势62.6%。跨球员测试（职业训练→业余评估）：击球类型82.9%（仅降0.8%），方向退化为多数类。World coordinates vs 图像空间坐标：跨球员击球类型从83%降至47%，方向从68%降至21%。

Conclusion: 基于3D world coordinates的击球类型识别具有较好的跨球员泛化能力，但方向预测难以迁移；验证了3D坐标对跨领域泛化的关键作用；所有代码可复现，可在Kaggle免费T4 GPU运行。

Abstract: We built a multi-task pipeline for tennis stroke biomechanics from plain RGB video. On top of pose-based stroke recognition, it adds two new tasks, predicting shot direction and grading posture quality, plus a rule-based feedback layer that suggests coaching tips. Strokes are found automatically using a weighted joint velocity score, s(t) = 0.5 v_wrist + 0.3 m_elbow + 0.2 m_shoulder, removing the need for manual annotation. Pose comes from MediaPipe Pose Landmarker (33 landmarks, metric world coordinates), with each stroke turned into a 30-frame by 39-feature sequence for TennisTransformerGPU, a compact 564,103-parameter transformer (4 layers, 4 heads, d=128) with three parallel output heads. Trained on 1,281 labeled strokes from 7 pros and 1 amateur across 11 videos, it hits 83.7% stroke-type accuracy, 61.9% on direction, and 62.6% on posture under a random 80/20 split. The interesting test is cross-player: train on pros, evaluate on the amateur. Stroke type barely budges, 82.9%, a 0.8% drop. Direction prediction does not transfer; it just falls back to the majority class. An ablation shows why world coordinates matter so much here: switching to image-space landmarks tanks cross-player stroke-type accuracy from 83% to 47% and direction from 68% to 21%. Everything runs on Kaggle's free T4 GPU tier and is fully reproducible.

</details>


### [161] [Stringalign: Moving beyond summary statistics with a transparent Unicode-aware tool for evaluating automatic transcription models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16015)
*Yngve Mardal Moe,Marie Roald*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stringalign 是一个用于自动转录（HTR/OCR/ASR）评估的 Python 库，解决了 CER/WER 指标定义模糊和不可复现的问题，提供透明预处理、错误类型分析和可视化工具，并遵循 FAIR 原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字符串比较指标（CER/WER）因字符/单词定义不统一而导致结果模糊、不可复现，且缺乏对错误类型的深入分析，无法有效指导模型选择和改进。

Method: 开发轻量级 Python 库 Stringalign，核心围绕：(1) 透明且可复现的预处理（归一化+分词）；(2) 提供 CER/WER 计算及错误类型（插入/删除/替换）分析的可视化工具；(3) 遵循 FAIR 原则设计，便于集成到现有工作流。

Result: 通过示例展示 Stringalign 解决了现有工具结果不透明、歧义性强的问题，提供了明确易用的替代方案。

Conclusion: Stringalign 为转录评估提供了更透明、可复现且功能更丰富的工具，有助于提升评估流程的标准化和可解释性。

Abstract: Comparing text strings is crucial when evaluating and understanding the performance of various text processing tasks such as document recognition and audio transcription. With an increasingly complex landscape of AI-based handwritten text recognition (HTR), optical character recognition (OCR) and automatic speech recognition (ASR) models, there is a need for tools that facilitate evaluation in a flexible and reproducible way. This paper presents Stringalign, a Python library designed to simplify the evaluation process for automatic transcription projects and facilitate transparent evaluation. Stringalign's tools to examine and visualise both the rate of errors and the types of errors a model makes, give insights into possible improvements and help inform model selection for a particular task. Widely used string comparison metrics, such as the character and word error rates (CER and WER), although useful, can be ambiguous due to varying definitions of what constitutes a character and a word. Stringalign addresses this challenge by ensuring all preprocessing (i.e. normalisation and tokenisation) is transparent and easily replicable, and by providing tools to move beyond summary statistics and analyse common model errors. Moreover, Stringalign adheres to FAIR (Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, and Reusable) principles for research software while staying lightweight and easy to adapt into researchers existing workflows. In this paper, we discuss challenges with character and word level string comparisons and show through examples that where existing tools can yield opaque and sometimes confusing results, Stringalign provides an easy-to-use and unambiguous alternative.

</details>


### [162] [The Third Challenge on Image Denoising at NTIRE 2026: Methods and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16031)
*Lei Sun,Hang Guo,Bin Ren,Shaolin Su,Xian Wang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Radu Timofte,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NTIRE 2026挑战赛图像去噪赛道（高噪声σ=50）报告，116支注册队伍中20支进入决赛，评测无约束条件下先进神经架构的峰值PSNR性能，汇总当前最先进的图像复原技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高噪声环境下（σ=50），探索不受参数或计算开销限制的先进神经网络架构，以最大化图像恢复的峰值量化性能（PSNR），推动图像去噪的SOTA边界。

Method: 通过举办挑战赛，收集并基准测试116支参赛队伍中20支决赛队伍提出的先进神经架构，在AWGN退化图像上以PSNR为唯一指标进行排名，不设参数量或计算量限制。

Result: 20支决赛队伍提交了各自的最优去噪架构，报告汇总了当前无约束条件下的最先进技术方案及其PSNR性能。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛提供了无约束图像去噪方向的全面技术快照，展示了高噪声场景下神经架构的最新突破。

Abstract: This paper reports on the NTIRE 2026 Challenge on Image Denoising, specifically focusing on the high-noise regime ($σ= 50$). The competition investigates advanced neural architectures designed to restore high-fidelity details from images corrupted by additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN). Unlike constrained benchmarks, this track emphasizes peak quantitative performance, measured by Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), without limitations on parameter count or computational overhead. By synthesizing contributions from 20 finalist teams out of 116 registrants, this report benchmarks the latest technical innovations and provides a comprehensive snapshot of the current state-of-the-art in unconstrained image restoration.

</details>


### [163] [Trusting Right Predictions for Wrong Reasons: A LIME Based Analysis of Deep Learning Interpretability in Lung Cancer Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16036)
*Samarpan Poudel,Vladislav D Veksler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了CNN、ResNet50和Vision Transformer三种模型在肺癌CT分类上的表现，发现虽然所有模型预测一致性极高（>0.99），但LIME解释一致性极低（<0.26），表明预测准确率不能代表推理一致性，可解释性评估应作为独立验证标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肺癌分类深度学习研究主要关注预测准确性，缺乏对模型决策过程的深入检查。本研究旨在揭示模型间推理一致性是否与预测一致性匹配。

Method: 在IQ-OTH/NCCD肺癌CT数据集上训练CNN、ResNet50、ViT三种结构不同的模型，使用LIME进行可解释性分析，并引入双相关性框架测量模型对之间的预测一致性和解释一致性。

Result: ResNet50准确率98.61%，CNN 97.91%，ViT 93.75%，ROC-AUC均为0.99；预测相关性>0.99，但LIME解释相关性<0.26；错误分类样本关注区域集中在肺实质之外。

Conclusion: 预测一致性不能代理推理一致性，可解释性评估应作为临床AI系统的独立验证标准。

Abstract: Lung cancer is the leading cause of cancer-related mortality, with approximately 2.5 million new cases and 1.8 million deaths annually, making reliable diagnosis a clinical priority. Although deep learning models have achieved strong performance in lung cancer classification, evaluation has largely focused on predictive accuracy, leaving their decision-making processes insufficiently examined. This study compares three architecturally distinct models: a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), a pretrained ResNet50, and a Vision Transformer (ViT), trained on the IQ-OTH/NCCD lung cancer CT dataset. Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) were applied to investigate model reasoning. In addition to standard performance metrics, a dual-correlation framework was introduced to measure both prediction agreement and explanation agreement across model pairs. All three models achieved strong classification performance, with ResNet50 attaining 98.61% accuracy, CNN 97.91%, and ViT 93.75%, while all achieved ROC-AUC scores of 0.99. Prediction correlations exceeded 0.99 across all model pairs, indicating highly consistent outputs. However, LIME explanation correlations remained below 0.26, revealing substantial differences in the image regions used to reach those predictions. Analysis of misclassified samples further identified a consistent spatial pattern: incorrect predictions were associated with attention outside the lung parenchyma, whereas correct predictions focused primarily within lung regions. These findings demonstrate that prediction agreement is a poor proxy for reasoning consistency, and that interpretability evaluation must be treated as an independent validation criterion alongside predictive performance in clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [164] [PointDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Scene Completion in the Point Cloud Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16048)
*Chidera Agbasiere,Mikhail Sannikov,Faith Ogunwoye,Erik Shaikhiev,Alex Kozinov,Ilya Mikhalchuk,Iana Zhura,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多token高斯VAE+交叉注意力池化实现场景级LiDAR压缩，结合锚点ICP真值去漂移，实现单步扩散补全模型，在SemanticKITTI上平方Chamfer距离降低16倍，推理延迟降低25-143倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏LiDAR点云稠密重建方法存在三大问题：(1) 目标级自编码器在室外尺度下坍塌为不稳定全局表示；(2) 地面真值被里程计漂移污染，系统性降低监督质量；(3) 多步扩散推理延迟过高，无法实时部署。

Method: (1) 多token高斯VAE + 交叉注意力池化，实现稳定场景级LiDAR压缩；(2) 基于锚点的ICP真值精炼管道，消除里程计漂移噪声；(3) 无脚手架单步扩散补全模型。

Result: SemanticKITTI seq.08上平方Chamfer距离从0.396 m²降至0.024 m²（~16倍），超越LiDiff 17-19%、ScoreLiDAR 10-11%，推理延迟降低25-143倍。

Conclusion: 数据质量在该任务中主导模型设计，多token潜空间为潜扩散场景补全提供稳定的第一阶段。

Abstract: Reconstructing dense 3D scenes from sparse LiDAR point clouds is a fundamental challenge in autonomous driving, where latent diffusion models offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches rely on object-level autoencoders that collapse into unstable global representations at outdoor scale and suffer from ground truth data corrupted by odometry drift that systematically degrades supervision quality. Furthermore, multi-step diffusion inference incurs prohibitive latency for real-time deployment. We propose a novel multi-token Gaussian VAE with cross-attention pooling for stable scene-scale LiDAR compression, combined with an anchor-based ICP ground truth refinement pipeline that eliminates drift-induced noise from training supervision. Together, these components enable a scaffold-free single-step diffusion completion model that achieves an approximately 16x reduction in squared Chamfer distance on SemanticKITTI seq. 08 (0.396 m^2 to 0.024 m^2), surpasses LiDiff and ScoreLiDAR by 17-19% and 10-11%, respectively, and operates at 25-143x lower inference latency. Our results demonstrate that data quality dominates model design in this regime and that multi-token latent spaces provide a stable first stage for latent diffusion-based scene completion.

</details>


### [165] [Stepwise Token Selection for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16067)
*Landi He,Shawn Young,Lijian Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将视觉token剪枝建模为序列决策过程，使用指针式选择机制迭代选取信息性token，并通过学习终止动作动态决定剪枝数量，替代传统固定比例剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法假设token独立且使用固定压缩比，导致剪枝策略无法适应不同输入的内容复杂度，需要更灵活的自适应剪枝方法。

Method: 将视觉token选择建模为序列决策过程，采用指针式选择机制（类似pointer network）逐步选取token，每一步依赖已选token；引入学习终止动作动态决定剪枝停止点；使用基于方差保持噪声插值的可微松弛，实现离散选择过程的端到端训练。

Result: 在LLaVA-v1.5-7B和Qwen2.5-VL-7B上始终优于固定比例基线；激进剪枝（去除88.9%视觉token）时保持94.6%原始准确率，prefill延迟加速1.88倍。

Conclusion: 将token剪枝从独立重要性评分+固定比例范式升级为自适应序列决策，显著提升了压缩效率与准确率之间的权衡。

Abstract: In multimodal large language models (MLLMs), inference cost is largely dominated by the visual token prefix rather than the language backbone, making token reduction a key factor for improving efficiency. Existing approaches typically assign independent importance scores to visual tokens and retain a fixed number of top-ranked tokens, implicitly assuming token independence and a uniform compression ratio across inputs. In this work, we reformulate visual token pruning as a sequential decision-making process. Specifically, we introduce a pointer-style selection mechanism that iteratively chooses informative tokens, conditioning each decision on previously selected ones, and dynamically determines when to stop via a learned termination action. This enables joint optimization of both the selected subset and its size. To enable end-to-end training under standard language modeling objectives, we design a differentiable relaxation based on a variance-preserving noise interpolation scheme, allowing gradients to propagate through the discrete selection process. Extensive experiments on LLaVA-v1.5-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms fixed-ratio baselines across different compression levels. Under aggressive pruning that removes 88.9% of visual tokens, our method preserves 94.6% of the original accuracy while achieving a 1.88x speed-up in prefill latency.

</details>


### [166] [Tool-IQA: Augmenting Image Quality Assessment with Simple Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16082)
*Guanyi Qin,Junjie Zhang,Chunming He,Yibing Fu,Jie Liang,Tianhe Wu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tool-IQA，将VLM的图像质量评估从静态单次打分转变为工具增强的工作流，使用放大镜检查局部细节、伽马校正器揭示隐藏伪影，并引入批量感知训练策略来奖励有效的工具调用，在多个IQA基准上达到SOTA（如CLIVE上PLCC 0.854）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM用于IQA的方法采用静态一次性评分范式，缺乏人类动态视觉检查过程（如选择性调整视角验证细节和伪影），导致两个问题：仅全局尺度感知限制了局部细节评估；原始强度分布可能淹没可见性，导致检查不充分。

Method: 提出Tool-IQA，包含：1) 简单但有效的视图工具：放大镜（检查局部细节）和伽马校正器（揭示可见性和隐藏伪影）；2) 结构化流水线：初始观察+笔记、工具增强深度检查、最终量化打分；3) 批量感知训练策略：奖励对评估有正向贡献的工具调用而非简单鼓励使用。

Result: 在多个IQA基准上显著超越现有SOTA模型，如CLIVE数据集上PLCC达到0.854。

Conclusion: 通过工具增强的工作流和有效的工具调用策略，将VLM的IQA能力从被动评分提升到主动检查，显著提高了评估准确性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been increasingly adopted for Image Quality Assessment (IQA). However, current methods typically employ a static one-shot scoring paradigm, despite the fact that humans assess image quality through dynamic visual inspection, e.g., selectively adjusting views to verify details and subtle artifacts. Specifically, relying solely on a single-pass observation introduces two primary limitations: first, perceiving the image only at a global scale restricts the assessment of finer local details; second, the original intensity distribution of the image may overwhelm the visibility, leading to insufficient inspection of image quality. To address these issues, we propose Tool-IQA, shifting the assessment mechanism from passive scoring to a tool-augmented workflow. In particular, we equip VLMs with simple yet effective view tools: a Magnifier to inspect local details, and a Gamma Corrector to uncover visibility and hidden artifacts. The assessment follows a structured pipeline that consists of an initial observation with rubric notes, a tool-augmented in-depth inspection, and a final quantification for calibrated quality score. Furthermore, to ensure efficient and purposeful tool callings, we introduce a batch-aware training strategy to reward tool interactions that can yield positive contributions rather than simply encouraging usage. Experiments on a variety of IQA benchmarks demonstrate that, with effective tool calling and calibrated assessment, our proposed Tool-IQA significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art models, e.g., it achieves a PLCC of 0.854 on the challenging CLIVE dataset.

</details>


### [167] [VinQA: Visual Elements Interleaved Long-form Answer Generation for Real-World Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16092)
*Young Rok Jang,Hyesoo Kong,Kyunghwan An,Jae Sub Huh,Gyeonghun Kim,Stanley Jungkyu Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VinQA 数据集和 M-GroSE 评估框架，研究多模态大模型在文档问答中生成图文交错长回答的能力，对比 Page Encoding 和 Modality Encoding 两种方法，发现开源模型 Qwen2.5-VL 微调后性能接近闭源前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档问答 MLLM 主要生成纯文本回答，未能有效利用文档中的表格、图表、照片等视觉元素。作者希望让模型能生成引用了视觉元素的长回答，并将视觉元素与文本交错呈现。

Method: 构建 VinQA 数据集（图文交错长回答+引用标注）；提出 Page Encoding（直接编码整页+边界框）和 Modality Encoding（分别提取文本和裁剪视觉元素）两种编码方式及对应的引用机制；提出 M-GroSE 评估框架（完整性、答案相关性、忠实性、不可回答性四维度）和 Visual Source F1 指标。

Result: 闭源前沿模型在 VinQA 上整体最优；微调 Qwen2.5-VL 显著缩小差距；Modality Encoding 在复杂文档上初始更鲁棒，但微调后 Page Encoding 可达到相当水平；Visual G-Eval 确认微调模型能在语义恰当位置插入视觉元素。

Conclusion: 开源模型可通过微调在图文交错文档回答任务上接近闭源模型，且简单的 Page Encoding 在训练后能够匹敌更复杂的 Modality Encoding，说明直接编码整页图像是一种有潜力的方案。

Abstract: Real-world documents combine text with tables, charts, photographs, and diagrams arranged in diverse layouts, yet existing research on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for document QA predominantly produces text-only responses, underutilizing these visual elements. We introduce VinQA, a dataset for long-form answer generation where cited visual elements are explicitly interleaved with their supporting text and grounded in relevant document pages. To support this task, we study two encoding methods for feeding raw document page images into an MLLM, along with their visual-element citation mechanisms: (1) Page Encoding, which directly encodes full-page images with bounding boxes of visual elements and treats these boxed regions as citable units; and (2) Modality Encoding, which parses each page to extract text and crop visual elements, encodes them separately, and uses these cropped elements as citable units. In our experiments, we propose M-GroSE, a multimodal evaluation framework extending GroUSE to assess answers along four dimensions: completeness, answer relevancy, faithfulness, and unanswerability. We additionally report Visual Source F1 to directly measure visual citation accuracy. Although proprietary frontier models still achieve the best overall scores on the VinQA test split, fine-tuning open Qwen2.5-VL models on the training split substantially improves their performance and narrows this gap. Modality Encoding is initially more robust for complex documents with long text, many visual elements, and diverse citation requirements. After training on VinQA, however, Page Encoding reaches a comparable level, competing effectively even without the explicit parsing used in Modality Encoding. Finally, Visual G-Eval, an MLLM-based judge, confirms that fine-tuned models insert visual elements at semantically appropriate positions with faithful supporting text.

</details>


### [168] [SceneCraft: Interactive System for Image Editing via Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16103)
*Duc-Manh Phan,Ngoc-Dai Tran,Duy-Khang Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneCraft是一个交互式框架，通过将图像表示为可编辑场景图，让用户直接操控视觉图来进行复杂的空间和关系操作，自动生成精准的编辑提示词，从而解决现有生成式AI在复杂多物体场景下依赖精确文本提示词的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI进行复杂场景图像编辑时，用户需要反复尝试和调整精确的文本提示词，缺乏结构化控制手段，认知负担大、效率低。

Method: 将图像表示为可编辑的场景图，用户直接对图进行空间/关系操作，图修改自动翻译为上下文感知的编辑提示词，并分发到多个生成模型执行。

Result: 相比传统文本提示词方法，SceneCraft提供更直观的控制机制，显著降低用户认知负担，生成质量与保真度获得用户更高评分。

Conclusion: 场景图驱动的交互式框架能有效弥合用户意图与模型执行之间的鸿沟，为复杂图像编辑提供更结构化、更直观的控制方式。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have enabled natural language-driven image editing, yet existing systems often fail in complex scenes with multiple interacting objects because they rely heavily on users crafting precise text prompts. To address the absence of structured control, we propose SceneCraft, a novel interactive framework that bridges user intent and model execution by representing images as editable scene graphs. Instead of guessing text prompts through trial and error, users interact directly with a visual graph to perform complex spatial and relational operations. These graph modifications are automatically translated into precise, context-aware editing prompts, effectively eliminating linguistic ambiguity. To ensure robust and diverse results, structured prompts are dispatched to multiple state-of-the-art generative models. Evaluations across diverse editing scenarios show that SceneCraft provides a more intuitive control mechanism, significantly reducing the cognitive burden of manual prompt engineering while generating outputs that users consistently rate as higher in quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [169] [EdgeZSAD: Practical Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16119)
*Taewan Cho,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EdgeZSAD，一种轻量级零样本异常检测系统，基于TinyViT-21M-512骨干网络、非对称全局-局部读出模块（EdgeGLR）和可复现的源端训练方案（Real-IAD-DR），在六个工业基准上达到与ViT-L大模型可比的性能，且可直接部署在Jetson Orin Nano Super和RB5 Gen2等边缘设备上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测需要零样本异常检测（ZSAD）在边缘部署约束下仍然有效，但现有方法依赖ViT-L等约300M参数的庞大骨干网络，超出典型嵌入式硬件的内存和算子预算。

Method: 以TinyViT-21M-512为骨干设计紧凑系统EdgeZSAD，提出非对称全局-局部读出模块（EdgeGLR），并构建可复现的源端训练协议Real-IAD-DR。采用源训练、目标不可见的协议，训练单一checkpoint并在六个工业基准上评估。

Result: MVTec-AD平均图像AUROC 91.6，VisA平均88.2；可直接部署于Jetson Orin Nano Super（TensorRT FP16）和RB5 Gen2（QNN GPU FP16）；六设备重评分基准图像AUROC漂移低于0.2个点，导出图在部署环境中保持主机端排序行为。

Conclusion: EdgeZSAD证明可以通过紧凑骨干网络和精心设计的组件在边缘设备上实现工业级零样本异常检测，性能与大规模ViT-L方法相当，且具有实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Industrial inspection needs zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) that remains useful under edge deployment constraints. Recent methods often rely on ViT-L foundation backbones (~300M parameters), which exceed the memory and operator budget of typical embedded hardware. We study this regime through EdgeZSAD, a compact reference system built around a TinyViT-21M-512 backbone, an asymmetric global-local readout (EdgeGLR), and a reproducible source-side training recipe (Real-IAD-DR). We train a single checkpoint in a source-trained, target-unseen protocol and evaluate it across six industrial benchmarks. Across three independent runs, the resulting model reaches an average image AUROC of 91.6 on MVTec-AD and 88.2 on VisA, while remaining directly deployable on Jetson Orin Nano Super (TensorRT FP16) and RB5 Gen2 (QNN GPU FP16). Across the six device-rescored benchmarks, image-AUROC drift stays below 0.2 points, indicating that the exported graph preserves host-side ranking behavior in the evaluated deployment setting.

</details>


### [170] [Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Visual Grounding for Remote Sensing Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16124)
*Ke Li,Di Wang,Yongshan Zhu,Ting Wang,Weiping Ni,Tao Lei,Quan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RSVG-ZeroOV，一种无需训练的零样本开放词汇遥感视觉定位框架，利用冻结的基础模型（VLM+DM）通过"概览-聚焦-演进"范式逐步生成精确的目标掩码，并扩展至视频时序定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSVG方法依赖昂贵的人工标注且难以泛化到开放词汇场景（新物体、细粒度属性、复杂空间关系等），缺乏一种无需微调/训练就能处理开放词汇查询的通用框架。

Method: 三步范式：(1) Overview：用VLM跨注意力图提取语义关联；(2) Focus：用扩散模型的细粒度先验补偿物体结构与形状信息；(3) Evolve：注意力演进模块抑制无关激活获得纯净掩码。视频扩展增加了关键帧选择器+时序传播器。

Result: 在6个图像和视频基准上，零样本设置下全面超越现有零样本基线，与弱监督/全监督方法相比达到竞争或更优性能。

Conclusion: 证明了无需训练的冻结基础模型组合（VLM+DM）可以有效解决开放词汇遥感视觉定位问题，为遥感领域提供了一种低成本、高泛化性的新范式。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual grounding (RSVG) aims to localize a referred target in a remote sensing image or video according to a natural language expression. Existing RSVG methods usually rely on task-specific manual annotations, which are costly to collect and inevitably limited in covering the diversity of real-world geospatial scenarios. As a result, they often struggle to generalize to open-vocabulary queries involving novel objects, fine-grained attributes, complex spatial relationships, and functional semantics. In this paper, we propose RSVG-ZeroOV, a training-free framework that leverages frozen generic foundation models for zero-shot open-vocabulary RSVG. RSVG-ZeroOV follows an Overview-Focus-Evolve paradigm, which exploits the distinct yet complementary attention patterns of vision-language models (VLMs) and diffusion models (DMs) to progressively generate precise grounding results. Specifically, (i) Overview utilizes a VLM to extract cross-attention maps that capture semantic correlations between the referring expression and visual regions; (ii) Focus leverages the fine-grained modeling priors of a DM to compensate for object structure and shape information often overlooked by VLM attention; and (iii) Evolve introduces a simple yet effective attention evolution module to suppress irrelevant activations, yielding purified object masks. To handle video inputs, we further present Video RSVG-ZeroOV, which extends image-level grounding to spatio-temporal grounding through a query-relevant key-frame selector and a temporal propagator, enabling efficient and temporally coherent video grounding without video annotations or fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on six image and video grounding benchmarks show that RSVG-ZeroOV consistently outperforms existing zero-shot baselines and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with weakly- and fully-supervised methods.

</details>


### [171] [Shift-and-Sum Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16131)
*Jaehyeon Moon,Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉自回归模型（VAR）的后训练量化（PTQ）框架，解决注意力值乘积重构误差大和码本采样频率与预测概率不匹配两大挑战，提出移位求和量化与校准数据重采样策略，在图像生成、修复等任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ在VAR模型上尚未被充分探索，直接应用存在注意力值乘积重构误差大（尤其在粗尺度高注意力分数区域）以及校准数据有限导致码本采样频率与预测概率不匹配的问题，亟需专门适配VAR的PTQ方法。

Method: (1) 移位求和量化（Shift-and-Sum Quantization）：对值令牌进行对称移位复制后分别量化再聚合，降低重构误差。(2) 校准数据重采样策略：使码本条目的采样频率与其预测概率对齐。

Result: 在类别条件图像生成、图像修复、外绘、类别条件编辑等任务上，在不同VAR架构上均取得一致改进，达到PTQ for VAR的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 提出的PTQ框架有效解决了VAR量化中的两大核心难题，验证了PTQ在视觉自回归模型上的可行性，为高效部署VAR模型提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment of deep networks using a small set of data. Its application to visual autoregressive models (VAR), however, remains relatively unexplored. We identify two key challenges for applying PTQ to VAR: (i) large reconstruction errors in attention-value products, especially at coarse scales where high attention scores occur more frequently; and (ii) a discrepancy between the sampling frequencies of codebook entries and their predicted probabilities due to limited calibration data. To address these challenges, we propose a PTQ framework tailored for VAR. First, we introduce a shift-and-sum quantization method that reduces reconstruction errors by aggregating quantized results from symmetrically shifted duplicates of value tokens. Second, we present a resampling strategy for calibration data that aligns sampling frequencies of codebook entries with their predicted probabilities. Experiments on class-conditional image generation, inpainting, outpainting, and class-conditional editing show consistent improvements across VAR architectures, establishing a new state of the art in PTQ for VAR.

</details>


### [172] [A Comprehensive Survey of Medical Image Segmentation: Challenges, Benchmarks, and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16153)
*Pengyu Zhu,Xiaojing Zhang,Kunbo Zhang,Chunyan Zhang,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对基于U-Net、Transformer和SAM架构的医学图像分割方法进行了系统性综述，涵盖公开数据集、代表性方法、评估指标及主要挑战，并提供了统一的比较分析框架和相关资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述多聚焦单一模型族或特定临床任务，缺乏对U-Net、Transformer、SAM三大主流的统一比较框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统梳理这些方法在提升分割精度与效率方面的有效性，以指导未来研究并促进临床转化。

Method: 将U-Net、Transformer和SAM三类方法纳入统一分析框架，从公共数据集、代表性方法、评估指标差异、多视角挑战等维度进行系统梳理和对比。

Result: 提供了一个综合性的文献综述，并公开了相关资源仓库（GitHub），但未提出新方法或实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文为医学图像分割领域提供了全面的系统性回顾和统一分析框架，有助于指导未来研究方向，但属于综述性质，缺乏具体方法创新和实验证据。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation plays a critical role in clinical diagnostics, treatment planning, disease monitoring, and neurological disorder identification. This article presents a comprehensive review of its systematic development, covering widely used public datasets, representative methods built on the U-Net, Transformer, and SAM architectures, and key evaluation metrics with their differences, followed by an analysis of major challenges from multiple perspectives. Unlike surveys that focus on a single model family or a specific clinical application, this review organizes U-Net-, Transformer-, and SAM-based methods within a unified analytical framework, with a particular focus on their effectiveness in improving segmentation accuracy and efficiency. This work aims to guide future research and support clinical translation of medical image segmentation, with all related resources publicly available in our GitHub repository: https://github.com/andrew-pengyu/Awsome_MedSeg/tree/main.

</details>


### [173] [Focus When Necessary: Adaptive Routing and Collaborative Grounding for Training-Free Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16158)
*Yifan Wang,Peiming Li,Shiyu Li,Zhiyuan Hu,Xiaochen Yang,Wenming Yang,Yang Tang,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LazyMCoT，一种无需训练的动态框架，通过自适应路由机制（基于首次token统计的预测不确定性评估）区分简单/困难样本，对困难样本使用协同定位模块（跨模态注意力+外部视觉专家）进行两阶段精炼，在提升推理精度的同时降低平均推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理高分辨率复杂图像时难以感知细粒度细节，而训练-free的图像缩放和局部裁剪方法存在计算冗余（对简单查询）和精度下降（截断全局上下文或引入背景噪声）的问题。

Method: 1) Adaptive Routing: 基于单次前向传播的首token统计量评估预测不确定性，通过保形校准确保困难样本召回，简单样本直接绕过。2) Collaborative Grounding: 对困难样本，先利用模型自身的跨模态注意力，再借助外部视觉专家进行两阶段精炼，生成精确的局部显示以恢复小或遮挡目标。

Result: 在多个基准上，LazyMCoT在提升推理精度的同时降低了平均推理延迟，性能可与训练-based方法相媲美，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过动态分配视觉感知计算量，实现了效率和精度的双赢，验证了无需额外训练的样本自适应策略在MLLM细粒度视觉推理中的有效性。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in cross-modal reasoning, they often struggle to perceive fine-grained details in complex high-resolution images. Recent training-free methods address this through image scaling and localized cropping. However, applying these manipulations indiscriminately introduces computational redundancy for simple queries and can degrade accuracy by truncating essential global context or introducing irrelevant background noise. To this end, we propose LazyMCoT, a dynamic and training-free framework that adaptively allocates visual grounding efforts based on sample difficulty. The framework features an Adaptive Routing mechanism that evaluates predictive uncertainty using first-token statistics from a single forward pass. This efficiently bypasses confident cases while ensuring the recall of difficult samples via conformal calibration. For these challenging cases, a Collaborative Grounding module integrates the inherent cross-modal attention of the model with an external visual expert through a two-stage refinement process. This refinement process generates a precise localized display to recover small or occluded targets. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that LazyMCoT rivals training-based approaches by simultaneously improving reasoning accuracy and reducing average inference latency. Our code is availble at https://github.com/TencentBAC/LazyMCoT.

</details>


### [174] [Continuous Splatting meets Retinex: Continuous Gaussian Splatting and Implicit Reflectance Modeling for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16159)
*Yuhan Chen,Yicui Shi,Guofa Li,Wenxuan Yu,Ying Fang,Guangrui Bai,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CGS-Retinex，首个基于显式-隐式联合建模的低光图像增强框架，融合连续高斯泼溅与Retinex理论，通过连续高斯渲染器估计全局光照分布、隐式神经表示建模反射率，实现了高质量低光增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光增强方法在平衡全局平滑光照调整与局部高频细节恢复时经常出现颜色失真和结构伪影。

Method: 将图像网格表示为连续参数场，用连续高斯渲染器估计空间连续全局光照分布；引入隐式神经表示独立建模反射率，利用浅层高频特征指导退化纹理细节重建；在Retinex框架内加入物理启发的亮度一致性约束和光照平滑正则化。

Result: 显著抑制暗区噪声和过曝，实现高频率结构保真度和颜色恢复，精确解耦光照与纹理。

Conclusion: 建立了低光图像增强的新型连续物理表示范式。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement aims to recover clear images from low-illumination observations and is crucial for high-level downstream vision tasks. However, existing methods frequently encounter color distortion and structural artifacts when balancing global smooth illumination adjustment and local high-frequency detail recovery. To address these issues, we propose CGS-Retinex as the first low-light image enhancement framework based on explicit-implicit joint modeling. Our framework deeply integrates continuous Gaussian splatting with Retinex theory. Specifically, we represent the image grid as a continuous parameter field and propose a continuous Gaussian renderer to estimate the spatially continuous global illumination distribution. This approach fundamentally eliminates grid artifacts caused by discrete Gaussian sampling. Furthermore, we introduce an implicit neural representation to model reflectance independently. We leverage shallow high-frequency features to guide the network in accurately reconstructing degraded texture details. Within the Retinex framework, we incorporate physics-inspired brightness consistency constraints and illumination smoothness regularization to enable explicit illumination and implicit reflectance to maintain proper exposure and achieve high-fidelity recovery of high-frequency structures and colors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CGS-Retinex significantly suppresses dark-region noise and overexposure while achieving exceptional high-frequency structural fidelity and color restoration by precisely decoupling illumination and texture. This work establishes a novel continuous physical representation paradigm for low-light image enhancement.

</details>


### [175] [Multimodal LLM-Empowered Re-Ranking for Generalizable Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16161)
*Jiachen Li,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出利用多模态大模型（MLLM）增强推理阶段重排序，以提升域泛化行人重识别性能。通过微调MLLM并设计域无关提示和难例挖掘，计算鲁棒的μ-距离来改进重排序，模型无关且可即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DG Re-ID方法聚焦于训练域泛化编码器，忽略了推理阶段的优化；传统重排序方法依赖编码器特征，在目标域上因域差距导致性能退化。欲利用MLLM的强泛化能力来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 1) 对MLLM进行监督微调，融入域无关提示和查询-候选难例挖掘；2) 在推理阶段用微调后的MLLM计算μ-距离（对域差距鲁棒）；3) 将μ-距离集成到传统重排序框架中，改进初始排序列表。

Result: 在多个DG Re-ID基准上取得一致且显著的性能提升，代码已开源。

Conclusion: MLLM赋能的距离度量能有效提升域泛化行人重识别的重排序效果，方法模型无关且易于集成。

Abstract: Domain Generalizable (DG) person re-identification (Re-ID) has attracted growing research interest due to its potential for deployment in unseen real-world scenarios. Most existing approaches address DG Re-ID by focusing on training domain-generalizable encoders but ignore the possible refinements in inference stage. In contrast, this work explores an alternative direction which improves inference re-ranking to enhance DG Re-ID. Conventional re-ranking methods typically rely on neighborhood-based distances to refine the initial ranking list, inherently depending on features produced by the Re-ID encoder. However, they deteriorate on target domains since the encoder lacks sufficient generalizability to produce reliable feature distances on unseen scenarios. Inspired by the remarkable generalization capabilities of recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we propose an MLLM-empowered distance metric to improve re-ranking in DG Re-ID. Specifically, we first adapt an MLLM to Re-ID data through supervised fine-tuning, which incorporates a domain-agnostic prompt and a query-candidate hard mining scheme. Then, the adapted MLLM is employed to compute a $μ$-distance during inference, which is robust to domain gap and significantly enhances subsequent re-ranking performance. Our approach is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into previous re-ranking frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently yields substantial performance improvements across multiple DG Re-ID benchmarks. The code of this work will be released at https://github.com/RikoLi/MUSE soon.

</details>


### [176] [Dehaze-GaussianImage: Zero-Shot Dehazing via Efficient 2D Gaussian Splatting Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16163)
*Yuhan Chen,Wenxuan Yu,Guofa Li,Kunyang Huang,Ying Fang,Yicui Shi,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Dehaze-GaussianImage，首个将2D高斯泼溅(2DGS)引入图像去雾的零样本框架，通过将雾霾图像建模为连续可演化各向异性高斯场，结合大气散射模型嵌入高斯参数空间的解耦学习策略，实现无监督SOTA去雾效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图去雾方法受限于像素级优化的计算冗余和隐式神经网络缺乏物理可解释性，难以平衡表示效率与重建保真度。

Method: 1) 将雾霾图像建模为2D高斯场替代传统像素网格范式；2) 提出重建-解耦零样本学习策略，将大气散射模型嵌入高斯参数空间；3) 高斯原语自适应分裂/克隆/剪枝实现几何级解耦；4) 显式结构保持约束抑制伪影。

Result: 在完全无监督、极少数量的参数下实现SOTA去雾性能。

Conclusion: 显式高斯表示在低级视觉任务中具有巨大潜力，为突破传统像素级处理范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Existing single image dehazing methods are often constrained by computational redundancy in pixel-level optimization and the lack of physical interpretability in implicit neural networks. These limitations hinder the balance between representation efficiency and reconstruction fidelity. To address these issues, we propose Dehaze-GaussianImage, the first zero-shot framework that introduces 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) into the image dehazing domain to break the traditional pixel-grid processing paradigm. Distinct from static convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or Transformers, our approach models hazy images as continuous and dynamically evolvable anisotropic Gaussian fields. Specifically, we propose a novel reconstruction-decoupling zero-shot learning strategy that embeds the atmospheric scattering model into the Gaussian parameter space. This strategy drives Gaussian primitives to adaptively split, clone, and prune during optimization, achieving geometric-level decoupling of the transmission medium and clear textures. Furthermore, explicit structure-preserving constraints are introduced to suppress artifacts commonly caused by traditional physical priors. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in a fully unsupervised manner with minimal parameters, highlighting the potential of explicit Gaussian representation for low-level vision tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Fi-Gaussian: Frequency-Aware Implicit Gaussian Splatting for Single Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16168)
*Yuhan Chen,Ying Fang,Guofa Li,Wenxuan Yu,Yicui Shi,Kunyang Huang,Wenbo Chu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fi-Gaussian，一种频率感知的隐式高斯泼溅网络用于单图像去雾。核心是频率感知隐式高斯泼溅模块，在频域解耦低频结构和高频纹理信息并用复值权重进行自适应高斯聚合恢复细节；同时引入物理驱动的散射重归一化机制估计透射图和大气光。在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像去雾面临高频细节丢失和物理散射建模困难两大问题，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 隐式高斯泼溅(Implicit Gaussian Splatting)在2D特征空间建模清晰图像的连续表示；2) 频率感知模块在频域解耦低频结构/高频纹理，用复值权重自适应高斯聚合；3) 物理驱动的散射重归一化机制，以隐式高斯先验引导估计透射图和大气光。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上定量SOTA，视觉质量优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 验证了隐式高斯泼溅在低级视觉任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Single image dehazing continues to be hindered by the loss of high-frequency details and the difficulty of accurate physical scattering modeling. To address these issues, we propose Fi-Gaussian, a frequency-aware implicit Gaussian splatting network for single image dehazing. Unlike explicit rendering methods that rely on 3D point clouds, our method employs implicit Gaussian splatting to adaptively model the underlying distribution of clear images as a continuous representation in 2D feature space. The core of the network is a frequency-aware implicit Gaussian splatting module, which decouples low-frequency structural information and high-frequency texture information in the frequency domain and then performs adaptive Gaussian aggregation with complex-valued weights to recover fine details. In addition, a physics-driven scattering renormalization mechanism is introduced to estimate the transmission map and atmospheric light under the guidance of implicit Gaussian priors. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that Fi-Gaussian achieves state-of-the-art quantitative performance and produces visually superior dehazed results, validating the effectiveness of implicit Gaussian splatting for low-level vision tasks.

</details>


### [178] [To forget is to preserve: Machine Unlearning for 3D medical image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16180)
*Nitesh Kumar Singh,Akhilesh Singh,Arjun Arora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于GDPR的机器学习模型遗忘策略，在MRBrainS18数据集上使用3D ResNet-50架构评估了四种近似遗忘方法，发现Noisy Label策略在50个epoch后在遗忘集上下降93%的同时保持了84%的保留集准确率，为基于主体的遗忘提供了性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDPR等数据隐私法规允许个人要求删除其在训练模型中的个人信息，亟需有效的模型遗忘(unlearning)策略来合规。

Method: 基于四种机制设计近似遗忘策略，使用预训练的3D ResNet-50 (Med3D框架) 在MRBrainS18数据集上进行医学图像分割任务评估，对比20和50 epoch两种训练时长下的Dice系数和MAE指标。

Result: Noisy Label策略表现最佳：遗忘集性能下降93%，保留集准确率保持84%（50 epoch）。其他策略在高epoch时遗忘过度且保留集性能出现灾难性退化。

Conclusion: 提供了基于主体级别的模型遗忘性能严格基线，为从业者选择合适的遗忘策略提供了明确标准。

Abstract: With new data privacy laws such as the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) [1] that allow individuals to ask that any of their personal information be erased from trained machine learning models, there has been a push to investigate the unlearning of data from models as a way to comply with these laws. In this regard, based on four mechanics, we consider several approximate unlearning strategies applied to the MRBrainS18 dataset [2]. We use a 3D ResNet-50 [3] as a backbone architecture for segmentation that has been pre-trained with the Med3D framework [4]. Considering the pre-trained model as a baseline, we evaluate respective retention accuracy on 2 types of subjects, i.e., retain and forget. We assess these approaches through their Dice similarity coefficient and mean absolute error (MAE) values using two separate training horizons 20 and 50 epochs. The results show that the Noisy Label strategy had the best overall trade-off with a decrease of 93% in the forget set while maintaining 84% accuracy for the retained set after 50 epochs. All other strategies showed extreme levels of forgetting at higher epoch numbers while also demonstrating catastrophic degradation of their retain set performance. The results of this study provide a strict baseline of performance metrics for unlearning on a subject-specific level and provide practitioners with clear criteria for selecting the proper strategies.

</details>


### [179] [Closed-Loop Triplet Synergistic Generation for Long-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16184)
*Xinlei Yin,Xiulian Peng,Xiao Li,Zhiwei Xiong,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoTriSyGen，一个基于智能体的闭环框架，通过视觉-文本-记忆三元组协同迭代修正，实现多镜头长视频生成中的跨镜头一致性和身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故事板驱动的长视频生成方法采用前馈式流程，缺乏利用已生成视觉证据反馈到后续条件生成中的机制，导致跨镜头身份漂移和累积不一致性。

Method: 构建闭环视觉-文本-记忆协同流程：1) 实体中心记忆（可变视觉状态，随故事演进持续更新）；2) VLM分析器对三元组（规划意图+持久记忆+生成视觉）进行推理；3) 两条路径：镜头内精炼（检测语义/构图违规触发重生成）和镜头间精炼（基于生成证据重写后续镜头提示词）。

Result: 在StoryBench基准上，相比现有代表性方法，在跨镜头一致性、提示遵循度和电影连续性方面取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 闭环反馈机制是解决长视频生成中累积不一致性的有效范式，实体中心记忆和多层次精炼策略是关键设计。

Abstract: Multi-shot long-form video generation remains challenging due to identity drift and compounding inconsistencies across shots. While storyboard-driven pipelines improve controllability, they are often executed in a feed-forward manner, with limited mechanisms to incorporate generated visual evidence back into subsequent conditioning. We propose CoTriSyGen, an agentic framework that formulates multi-shot long video generation as a closed-loop visual-text-memory synergy process, where planned intent, persistent memory, and generated visuals are jointly leveraged for iterative correction and long-range coherence. A vision-language-model-based analyzer reasons over this triplet and produces updates to both prompts and memory along two pathways: (i) intra-shot refinement, which triggers targeted regeneration when semantic or compositional violations are detected and refines image-to-video prompt for coherent motions; and (ii) inter-shot refinement, which rewrites subsequent-shot prompts to propagate newly manifested entities or attributes and improve prompt quality (e.g., compositional grounding and cinematic fluency) based on generated evidence. The loop is grounded in an entity-centric memory modeled as a mutable visual state that evolves as the story progresses, which is continuously updated by both the generator and the analyzer by adding new and evolved entities to reflect appearance changes, accumulated multi-view evidence, and multi-entity compositions.
