Result: 仿真消融验证了关键设计选择的重要性；真实世界实验展示了零样本迁移，在不同初始姿态、风扰动和额外负载下均能实现鲁棒恢复。

Conclusion: 敏捷四旋翼跌倒恢复可以在没有显式状态估计的情况下，仅使用有限且不可靠的机载传感实现。

Abstract: Autonomous fall recovery is a critical capability for quadrotors operating in real-world environments, where collisions or failures may leave the vehicle resting on the ground in an arbitrary attitude. This problem is challenging because recovery must be achieved under limited onboard sensing, in constrained free space, with ground contact, and in the presence of unknown disturbances. In this letter, we present an RL-based framework for autonomous fall recovery of a quadrotor from arbitrary ground attitudes to stable hover using only lightweight onboard sensors. To address severe partial observability and intermittent sensor invalidity, we train a recurrent policy within an asymmetric actor--critic architecture, leveraging an Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) controller to track the policy output. Combined with high-fidelity simulations of motor response and optical flow, the overall training framework significantly reduces the sim-to-real gap. Simulation ablation studies validate the importance of the main design choices, while real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot transfer and robust recovery under different initial attitudes, wind disturbances, and additional payloads. These results demonstrate that agile quadrotor fall recovery can be achieved without explicit state estimation using only limited and unreliable onboard sensing.

</details>


### [326] [ADAPT: Analytical Disturbance-Aware Policy Training for Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16542)
*Bofan Lyu,Jindou Jia,Kuangji Zuo,Yanshuo Lu,Shijia Han,Gen Li,Boyu Ma,Jingliang Li,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是一个框架，通过解析全身扰动观测器将物理驱动的外力/力矩估计注入人形机器人策略，无需力/扭矩传感器，在Unitree G1上实现了优于基线的扰动预测鲁棒性和速度跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的人形机器人力交互任务方法依赖广泛的域随机化、任务特定力目标或基于运动历史的学习力估计器，这些方法在精度、任务迁移性或分布外鲁棒性上存在妥协，缺乏物理可解释的通用扰动感知能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT框架，核心是一个解析全身扰动观测器，利用可获取的机器人动力学在线估计残余力/力矩（无需力传感器），并将估计的扰动直接输入策略网络，提供物理推导的外力感知。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上，ADAPT实现了准确的扰动预测，在躯干扰动、站立推力和不对称手部负载下比仅本体感觉基线具有更强鲁棒性，在分布外扰动下也展现出改进的速度跟踪，并能通过惩罚下肢关节的推断扰动来鼓励更轻快的运动。

Conclusion: 基于解析动力学的扰动观测器可以有效替代传感器或纯学习方法，为人形机器人提供可泛化的物理外力感知能力，提升力交互任务的鲁棒性和迁移性。

Abstract: Humanoids deployed in human-centered environments must handle force-interactive tasks, where external contacts introduce unexpected disturbances that disrupt locomotion accuracy and stability. Existing learning-based approaches rely on broad domain randomization, task-specific force objectives, or learning-based force estimators from motion history, each of which compromises accuracy, task transferability, or out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness. We present Analytical Disturbance-Aware Policy Training (ADAPT), a framework that equips humanoid policies with a physically grounded disturbance observer. The core of ADAPT is an analytical whole-body disturbance observer that estimates residual force/torque online with the accessible robot dynamics, without requiring force/torque sensors. Fed directly into the policy, the estimated disturbances give the humanoid an explicit, physics-derived sense of external force/torque that can generalize across diverse unseen scenes. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid show that ADAPT achieves accurate disturbance prediction and stronger robustness than a proprioception-only baseline under torso perturbations, standing pushes, and asymmetric hand payloads, with improved velocity tracking even on OOD disturbances. Moreover, ADAPT enables penalizing inferred disturbances at lower-body joints to encourage lighter locomotion.

</details>


### [327] [Elastic ODYN: Differentiable Optimization for Infeasible Control and Learning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16564)
*Aristotelis Papatheodorou,Jose Rojas,Ioannis Havoutis,Carlos Mastalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Elastic ODYN，一种基于平滑 ℓ2 弹性松弛的原对偶非内点 QP 求解器，可在不可行条件下保持良态性并收敛到最接近可行解；衍生出可微 QP 层（Elastic OdynLayer）和不可行感知 SQP（Elastic OdynSQP），在接触力学、参数辨识、四足/人形机器人轨迹优化中优于现有弹性 QP 求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统常面临冲突目标、建模误差和退化接触条件，导致 QP 不可行，现有求解器和可微 QP 层假设可行域非空，在约束无法同时满足时出现数值失败或梯度不稳定。

Method: 使用平滑 ℓ2 弹性松弛处理不可行性，构建原对偶非内点 QP 求解器；设计轻量精炼阶段恢复物理有意义的对偶变量；在此基础上开发可微 QP 层（Elastic OdynLayer）和不可行感知 SQP（Elastic OdynSQP）。

Result: 在基准 QP、奇异接触力学、可微参数辨识、四足/人形轨迹优化中，Elastic ODYN 在鲁棒性、热启动性能和收敛可靠性上一致优于现有弹性 QP 求解器。

Conclusion: 该方法使优化、仿真、控制和学习能够超越现有方法的可行性假设，适用于机器人在退化/不可行条件下的稳定求解。

Abstract: Robotic systems routinely encounter conflicting objectives, modeling errors, and degenerate contact conditions that render quadratic programs (QPs) infeasible. Yet most optimization solvers and differentiable QP layers assume feasibility, leading to numerical failures, unstable gradients, or solver breakdown when constraints cannot be simultaneously satisfied. We present Elastic ODYN, a primal--dual non-interior-point QP solver that handles infeasibility through smooth squared-$\ell_2$ elastic relaxations. The resulting formulation remains well posed under ill-conditioning and degeneracy, supports warm starting, and converges to closest-to-feasible solutions when no feasible point exists. A lightweight refinement stage recovers physically meaningful dual variables from the elastic solution. Building on this framework, we develop Elastic OdynLayer, a differentiable QP layer with stable gradients under infeasibility, and Elastic OdynSQP, an infeasibility-aware SQP method that resolves inconsistent subproblems and intrinsically infeasible optimal control tasks through selective constraint relaxation. We evaluate the framework on benchmark QPs, singular contact mechanics, differentiable parameter identification, and quadrupedal and humanoid trajectory optimization. Across all settings, Elastic ODYN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art elastic QP solvers in robustness, warm-start performance, and convergence reliability, enabling optimization, simulation, control, and learning beyond the feasibility assumptions of existing methods.

</details>


### [328] [Automated Digital Twin Construction for Highway Scenarios Using LiDAR Point Clouds and OpenStreetMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16570)
*Yongqi Zhao,Dong Bi,Paul Kovacevic,Tomislav Mihalj,Martin Schabauer,Johannes Betz,Arno Eichberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合LiDAR点云与OSM数据的自动化工作流，用于生成符合ASAM OpenDRIVE标准的高精度高速公路地图，实验显示横向RMSE为0.740m，可直接用于IPG CarMaker和Esmini等仿真平台，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从真实传感器数据构建标准化道路地图（如ASAM OpenDRIVE）耗时且昂贵，现有方法中LiDAR精度高但覆盖范围有限，OSM覆盖广但缺乏车道级几何精度，亟需一种融合两种数据源优势的自动化方案。

Method: 提出自动化工作流：从LiDAR点云重建主线道路几何，从OSM道路图推断匝道几何与拓扑，融合生成完整的高速公路互通式立交OpenDRIVE地图，最小化人工干预。

Result: 横向RMSE为0.740m，生成的地图可直接在IPG CarMaker和Esmini等主流仿真平台使用。

Conclusion: 验证了测量派生几何与地图派生拓扑相结合的方案在自动化OpenDRIVE数字孪生生成中的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate road environment modeling is fundamental to the simulation and validation of automated driving systems. However, constructing road maps in standardized formats such as ASAM OpenDRIVE from real-world sensor data remains a time-consuming and costly process. Mobile mapping LiDAR captures accurate lane-level geometry but is confined to the driven corridor, while OpenStreetMap (OSM) provides broad road network topology but lacks geometric precision at the lane level. To address this, an automated workflow is proposed to fuse LiDAR point clouds with OSM data to generate georeferenced ASAM OpenDRIVE maps of highway environments, requiring minimal manual intervention. The pipeline reconstructs mainline roads from LiDAR-derived measurements and infers ramp geometry and topology from the OSM road graph, enabling complete highway interchange modeling without full sensor coverage. Experiments demonstrate a mean lateral RMSE of 0.740 m, and the generated maps are directly usable in mainstream simulation platforms including IPG CarMaker and Esmini. These results validate the effectiveness of combining measurement-derived geometry with map-derived topology for automated OpenDRIVE digital twin generation. The project code is available at https://github.com/ftgTUGraz/opendrive-digital-twin-generator

</details>


### [329] [Steering Generative Reinforcement Learning into Stable Robotic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16572)
*Yixuan Wang,Shutong Ding,Ke Hu,Tianxiang Gui,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SteerGenPO，将训练好的生成式扩散/流策略（随机）通过潜空间强化学习转化为确定性鲁棒控制器，分离探索（随机采样）与控制（确定性潜变量预测）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略的随机性在高维机器人系统中会导致动作累积不一致、稳定性差，不适合精确控制，需要将随机策略转化为确定性控制器。

Method: 在潜空间中学习一个潜变量actor，替代训练好的生成式策略中的随机潜变量采样，使其输出状态依赖的确定性潜变量，从而将随机生成策略转变为确定性控制器。探索与控制分离：训练时用随机采样探索，部署时用确定性潜变量控制。

Result: 在6个Isaac Lab基准测试和Unitree G1运动任务上，SteerGenPO优于经典RL和生成式RL基线，且确定性潜变量控制带来更稳定的推理行为和更可靠的命令响应。

Conclusion: 潜空间RL框架可以有效弥合生成式策略的随机探索与确定性控制之间的鸿沟，实现稳定精确的机器人控制。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative policies provide a powerful policy class for reinforcement learning by inducing rich stochastic exploration through iterative action generation. However, the stochasticity of diffusion policies is not suitable for stable and precise control in high-dimensional robotic systems, where small action variations can accumulate into inconsistent motion and reduced robustness. To address this issue, we propose SteerGenPO, a latent-space reinforcement learning framework that steers a trained generative policy into a robust deterministic robotic controller. The key idea is to replace stochastic latent sampling of the trained generative policy with a learned latent actor that predicts a state-dependent latent input for the generative policies. This separates exploration and control: stochastic generative sampling provides diverse action proposals during policy learning, while deterministic latent steering provides stable and adaptive control at deployment. We evaluate SteerGenPO on six Isaac Lab benchmarks and a Unitree G1 locomotion task. The results show SteerGenPO improves over both classical RL and generative RL baselines, while its deterministic latent steering produces more stable inference-time behaviors and more reliable command responses.

</details>


### [330] [WaveSync: Constrained Wavefront Optimization for Synchronized Co-Speech Gestures in Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16600)
*Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Gia Huy Uong,Phuc Van Dinh,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Xiem HoangVan,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WaveSync提出了一种混合框架，利用大语言模型将对话解析为语义结构并构建连续语义重要性波，再通过动态运动基元（DMP）和波前优化在物理人形机器人上生成与语音节奏同步、符合运动学约束且富有表现力的共语手势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人形机器人由于严格的运动学和动力学约束，无法像虚拟化身那样自由执行快速或重叠运动，导致手势节奏必须与语音重音对齐这一同步问题与硬件安全运动规划相互耦合，现有方法难以同时兼顾表达性、语义一致性和运动学可行性。

Method: 1) LLM将对话响应分解为结构化语义模式并为每个词分配重要性权重，构建连续语义重要性波；2) DMP根据重要性波塑造手势轨迹，确保运动学可行性；3) 波前优化阶段对齐峰-峰手势-语音同步，通过手势持续时间压缩和前向传播解决残余运动学违规。

Result: 基于五个对话场景的实验表明，该方法在同步精度上表现优异，在客观和主观评估中均优于三种基线方法，且各组件都对生成富有表现力、语义一致且符合运动学约束的手势起到必要作用。

Conclusion: WaveSync有效解决了人形机器人共语手势生成中语义对齐与硬件约束耦合的问题，验证了LLM引导语义波与DMP运动规划相结合的可行性，代码和资源已开源。

Abstract: Expressive co-speech gestures are crucial for natural human-robot interaction, but generating them on physical humanoid robots is difficult because gesture strokes must align with speech emphasis while satisfying strict kinematic and dynamic constraints. Unlike virtual avatars, humanoid robots cannot freely execute rapid or overlapping motions, making word-level synchronization and hardware-safe motion planning a coupled problem. We present \textbf{WaveSync}, a hybrid framework in which a Large Language Model decomposes dialogue responses into structured semantic schemas and assigns per-word importance weights, constructing a continuous Semantic Importance Wave. Gesture trajectories are shaped through Dynamic Movement Primitives, enforcing kinematic feasibility while enhancing expressiveness. A Wavefront Optimization stage aligns peak-to-peak gesture-speech synchronization and resolves residual kinematic violations through gesture-duration compression and forward propagation. Experimental evaluation based on five dialogue scenarios shows that our method achieves high synchronization accuracy and outperforms three baselines in both objective and subjective evaluations. Each component in WaveSync plays a necessary role in producing gestures that are expressive, semantically grounded, and kinematically compliant. The code, resources, and videos are available at \href{https://github.com/pairs-lab/WaveSync}{WaveSync}

</details>


### [331] [Reinforcement Learning with Inner-loop Dynamics Estimator for Aerial Manipulation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16621)
*Shivansh Pratap Singh,Samaksh Ujjwal,Ishita Chaudhary,V R Vasudevan,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合强化学习与内环动力学估计器的分层控制框架，用于解决无人机在快速臂运动、负载变化等动态不确定性下的全身操作问题，在搭载3自由度机械臂的四旋翼平台上进行了硬件实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中操作器在快速臂运动、负载变化和动态不确定性下的全身直接操作问题尚未被有效解决，依赖精确耦合动力学模型的方法在复杂场景下局限性明显。

Method: 分层控制框架：RL外环将6-DOF末端执行器目标映射为全身协调命令，避免在策略层依赖精确耦合动力学模型；内环通过动力学估计器跟踪命令并补偿瞬态惯性偏移和不确定性，无需系统模型知识。

Result: 在定制四旋翼+3-DoF机械臂平台上进行硬件实验，与RL+PID和RL+INDI+PID基线相比，所提方法降低了末端执行器跟踪误差，提高了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将学习的全身协调与基于估计器的低层补偿相结合，能在变化操作条件下提高空中操作器的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators enable physical interaction in hard-to-reach environments; however, the combined problem of direct whole-body aerial manipulation under rapid arm motion, payload changes, and related unknown dynamic uncertainty remains a largely unsolved problem. We present a hierarchical control framework that combines Reinforcement Learning (RL) with an inner-loop dynamics estimator to address this problem. The RL outer loop maps desired 6-degrees-of-freedom (DOF) end-effector targets to coordinated whole-body commands, enabling direct task-driven control without relying on a fully accurate coupled dynamic model in the policy layer. An inner loop then tracks these commands while compensating for transient inertial shifts and uncertainty during execution via a dynamics estimator scheme without requiring system model knowledge. We validate the proposed approach on a custom quadrotor equipped with a 3-DoF manipulator through hardware experiments under varying payload conditions. Compared with RL+PID and RL+INDI+PID baselines, the proposed method reduces end-effector tracking error and improves task success rate across the tested hardware conditions. These results show that combining learned whole-body coordination with estimator-based low-level compensation improves the precision and robustness of aerial manipulation under changing operating conditions.

</details>


### [332] [PATCH: Action-Chunk-Conditioned Latent Patch Innovation Monitoring for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16690)
*Yanan Zhou,Ranpeng Qiu,Yincong Chen,Jiajie Cui,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PATCH是一个动作块条件化的潜在块创新监控器，用于部署时干预。它根据主动动作块定义投影执行走廊，预测内部潜在块演变，累积机器人自身运动无法解释的持续残差，形成局部干预信号，使PATCH-Router能够暂停执行、选择恢复源、并在局部创新消退后恢复原始策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时监控器依赖全局观测异常、策略不确定性或帧级视觉变化，难以区分任务相关的执行风险与良性视觉变化，导致在开放工作空间中的部署仍然脆弱。

Method: 定义动作块条件化的投影执行走廊，预测潜在块演变，累积残差作为局部干预信号，通过PATCH-Router进行暂停、恢复源选择与策略恢复。

Result: 在真实机器人数据上，PATCH产生比竞争监控器更稳定和上下文相关的触发信号；真实机器人部署验证了监控驱动的干预和策略恢复能力。

Conclusion: PATCH通过局部化创新残差实现了对任务相关扰动的精确感知和鲁棒干预，优于全局观测异常等基线方法。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies have made substantial progress in real-world robot manipulation, particularly for short-horizon action generation. However, deployment in open workspaces remains fragile under unexpected local scene dynamics, such as moving objects, transient occlusions, or disturbances near the intended motion. Existing runtime monitors often rely on global observation anomalies, policy uncertainty, or frame-level visual changes, and struggle to distinguish task-relevant execution risk from benign visual variation. We introduce PATCH, an action-chunk-conditioned latent patch innovation monitor for deployment-time intervention. Given the active action chunk, PATCH defines a projected execution corridor, predicts latent patch evolution inside it, and accumulates persistent residuals unexplained by the robot's own motion. These residuals form a localized intervention signal that allows PATCH-Router to pause execution, select an available recovery source, and resume the original policy once localized innovation subsides. Experiments on real robot rollout data show that PATCH produces more stable and context-relevant triggers than competing runtime monitors. Real-robot deployment further demonstrates monitor-driven intervention and policy resumption for disturbance-aware manipulation. Project Page: https://yananzhou5555.github.io/PATCH/.

</details>


### [333] [VENOM: Versatile Embodied Network for Omni-bodied Motion tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16696)
*Siddharth Padmanabhan,Kazuki Miyazawa,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VENOM是一个基于GPT的跨本体全身运动跟踪模型，利用多个人形机器人数据训练，无需将控制问题分解为上/下半身，在仿真中实现了与专家级强化学习方法相近的运动跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨本体全身运动跟踪方法大多将控制解耦为上下半身分别处理，且缺乏仅从示范数据实现专家级跟踪的研究，本文旨在解决这一未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 构建包含状态、动作和奖励的多个人形机器人运动跟踪数据集（VENOM dataset），采用GPT架构训练跨本体全身运动跟踪模型，不依赖上下半身控制分解。

Result: VENOM在不同人形机器人上实现了比单纯监督训练的MLP更稳定的运动跟踪，并且尽管没有奖励反馈，其跟踪能力与使用非对称actor-critic强化学习训练的专家模型接近。

Conclusion: 基于GPT的序列建模方法可以有效实现跨本体全身运动跟踪，无需任务分解或奖励信号，为仅从示范数据学习人形机器人全身控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving expert-level expressive full-body motion tracking across multiple humanoids solely from demonstration data remains a challenging and relatively an underexplored problem in humanoid robot learning. Cross-embodiment motion tracking policies are mostly trained by decoupling the control problem into upper and lower body control. This work proposes VENOM, a cross-embodiment full-body motion tracking model for humanoids in simulation. VENOM is a GPT-based motion tracker trained on multiple humanoid data that can track the entire body without the requirement to split into upper and lower body control. We curate a multi-humanoid motion tracking dataset called the VENOM dataset that contains states, actions, and rewards and train VENOM and the baselines on this dataset. In this letter, we evaluate VENOM's performance against baselines and show that we can achieve a stable motion tracker across different humanoids more capable than an MLP trained on multiple humanoid data with supervised learning alone, and also show that despite lack of reward feedback, VENOM closely matches the tracking capability of experts that were trained using asymmetric-actor critic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [334] [Pride and Prejudice: Toward an Information-Theoretic Framework for Mutually Communicative Driver Behavior Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16735)
*Tingjun Li,Nan Xu,Shuo Feng,Hassan Askari,Bruno Henrique Groenner Barbosa,Konghui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个结合Level-k贝叶斯说服博弈、主动信号、信息论奖励和自适应通信能力的框架，用于建模自动驾驶中AV与HV之间的隐式双向通信（换道场景），通过C-MIRL算法在NGSIM数据集上标定，相比无通信基线将强制换道预测误差降低20%，并通过驾驶员在环问卷验证了主观有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通中AV与HV常误读彼此意图，导致换道不安全、低效，现有工作缺乏对隐式双向通信（表达意图与探测偏好）及认知不确定性的显式建模。

Method: 1) Level-k贝叶斯说服博弈框架；2) 虚拟特征主动信号与信息论互通信奖励；3) 自适应通信能力权重；4) 提出Pride-Inquiry与Pride-Prejudice平面分析通信强度与倾向；5) 使用C-MIRL算法在NGSIM上标定模型参数。

Result: 强制换道预测误差降低20%；问卷得分与标定通信变量正相关；inquiry与listening贡献大于pride与expression；inquiry偏好驾驶员间差异更大。

Conclusion: 显式建模互通信与认知不确定性对交互式驾驶至关重要，inquiry/listening比单纯表达更关键。

Abstract: Mixed autonomy driving becomes unsafe and inefficient when autonomous vehicles (AVs) and human-driven vehicles (HVs) misread each other's intentions. We study this problem as implicit mutual communication in lane changes. The proposed framework models how the ego vehicle both expresses its intent and probes the other driver's preference under epistemic uncertainty. It combines a level-k Bayesian persuasion game with virtual features for proactive signaling, information-theoretic rewards for mutual communication, and adaptive weights of communication affordances. We further introduce the Pride-Inquiry (P-I) and Pride-Prejudice (P-P) planes to analyze communication intensity and tendency. The model is calibrated with a Communication-Based Multi-Agent Inverse Reinforcement Learning algorithm (C-MIRL) on the naturalistic NGSIM dataset. Compared with the non-communicative baseline, the proposed model reduces the prediction error of mandatory lane changes by up to 20% while maintaining strong generalization. Driver-In-the-Loop questionnaire scores are positively correlated with the calibrated communication variables, supporting the subjective validity of the model. The learned rewards further show that inquiry and listening affordances contribute more than pride and expression alone, and that inquiry preference varies more strongly across drivers. These results support explicit modeling of mutual communication and epistemic uncertainty in interactive driving.

</details>


### [335] [DataLadder: A Simulation-Enabled Interconversion Toolchain for the Embodied Data Pyramid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16776)
*Peidong Liu,Yongce Liu,Songyan Guo,Fuyuan Ma,Zhihao Yuan,Ao Li,Zengjue Chen,Wenhao Li,Tianle Zhang,Mingyang Li,Jiale Zhang,Junzhe Xiong,Zhiyuan Xiang,Dafeng Chi,Yuzheng Zhuang,Yihang Li,Qingrong He,Jiaming Liang,Chen Cai,Peng Hao,Mingxi Luo,Song Wang,Junwu Xiong,Ruodai Li,Liyi Luo,Wei Tan,Dongjiang Li,Jiawei Li,Hui Shen,Yicheng Gong,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DataLadder 是一个仿真驱动的双向转换工具链（Robot ⇄ Simulation ⇄ Human），一方面将真实机器人操作任务重建为数字化双胞胎用于可扩展评估（Robot→Simulation→Human），另一方面将人类示教数据提升到仿真中校验物理约束后转换为机器人可用数据（Human→Simulation→Robot），并打包为云服务基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人评估和数据采集成本高、速度慢、难以复现，亟需一种既能保持物理保真度又能规模化扩展的仿真-真实互转方案，实现对齐人类的模型评估与数据生成。

Method: 构建双向管道：(1) Robot→Simulation→Human：将真实桌面整理任务重建为校准的数字孪生，结合人类具身反馈检查仿真运动自然度；(2) Human→Simulation→Robot：将第一人称人类示教提升到仿真，在机器人物理约束下校验并转换为以机器人为中心的轨迹、标注和视觉观测。核心仿真器为JoySim，模块打包为JD Cloud云服务。

Result: 论文展示了双向转换管道的可行性，但摘要未给出具体定量实验结果（如评估指标、数据增益等），主要描述系统设计和云服务化部署。

Conclusion: DataLadder 提供了一个可复用的仿真-真实双向基础设施，同时支持对齐人类的可扩展评估和数据生成，但具体性能增益尚待验证。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies require trustworthy evaluation and robot-usable training data, but both are difficult to scale with physical robots alone. Real-robot trials and demonstrations remain the most faithful source of deployment signals, yet they are slow, costly, and hard to reproduce. We present DataLadder, a simulation-enabled interconversion toolchain for human-robot aligned model evaluation and data generation, denoted as Robot $\rightleftharpoons$ Simulation $\rightleftharpoons$ Human. On the one hand, the Robot $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Human pathway supports human-robot aligned model evaluation by reconstructing real-robot tabletop organization tasks as calibrated digital twins for scalable evaluation, while using human embodied feedback to inspect and refine the naturalness of simulated motions. On the other hand, the Human $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Robot pathway supports human-robot aligned data generation: it lifts ego-centric human demonstrations into simulation, checks them under robot physical constraints, and converts them into robot-centered trajectories, annotations, and visual observations. Together, these pathways use the JoySim simulator as both a scalable evaluation layer and a physical consistency filter for robot data generation. We further package the core reconstruction, simulation, rendering, and realism-augmentation modules as cloud services on JD Cloud, turning the system into reusable infrastructure for robot data generation and model evaluation.

</details>


### [336] [DIFF-IPPO: Diffusion-Based Informative Path Planning with Open-Vocabulary Belief Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16780)
*Sausar Karaf,Oleg Sautenkov,Mikhail Martynov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DIFF-IPPO，将开放词汇信念地图生成器与扩散规划器结合，用于非高斯信念地图上的全局信息路径规划；在模拟搜救场景中，5架无人机编队在3.5分钟内完成首次检测，检测得分81.49%-86.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息路径规划方法多依赖高斯过程信念模型，难以处理开放词汇语义感知产生的多模态非高斯信念地图；扩散模型虽擅长建模复杂分布，但在信息路径规划中应用有限。

Method: DIFF-IPPO：开放词汇信念地图生成器（结合视觉语言模型）→ 扩散规划器（以信念地图为条件生成全局轨迹）→ 多无人机编队批处理轨迹生成。

Result: 归一化检测得分81.49%-86.55%；模拟搜救场景中5架无人机3.5分钟首次发现燃烧建筑。

Conclusion: 扩散规划器可有效处理非高斯信念地图上的全局路径规划，在语义目标搜索任务中优于传统方法。

Abstract: Exploration and object search require robots to perceive their environment, identify regions of interest, and plan trajectories that improve target-detection likelihood or maximize information gain. Many IPP methods, especially in continuous environmental monitoring, rely on Gaussian-process belief models, while object-search settings often produce complex, multimodal belief maps from semantic or open-vocabulary perception. Global trajectory generation directly conditioned on such non-Gaussian belief maps remains comparatively underexplored. Although diffusion-based planners offer strong capabilities for modeling such distributions, their use in informative path planning remains limited. In this work, we propose DIFF-IPPO, a pipeline that integrates an open-vocabulary belief map generator with a diffusion-based planner for global trajectory generation over belief maps. The method generates trajectories that concentrate sensor coverage over high-belief regions, achieving normalized detection scores between 81.49% and 86.55% across different dataset scenarios. We validate the system in a simulated search-and-rescue scenario where the planner searches candidate building regions to locate a burning building. In this setting, a team of five drones using batched belief-map-conditioned trajectory generation achieves first detections in 3.5 minutes.

</details>


### [337] [SoK: Security and Privacy of Foundation-Model-Powered Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16788)
*Xueluan Gong,Chen Chen,Jinxin Liu,Qian Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个渐进式F-E-S-G结构边界框架，用于分析大模型赋能机器人系统中的安全与隐私风险。该框架包含四个层次：基础模型层(F)、具身系统层(E)、支撑生态系统层(S)和治理影响层(G)，并基于此构建多层次分类法，系统化梳理了96篇论文，揭示了跨边界威胁模式、防御不匹配和评估缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献综述通常聚焦于特定FM类型、风险类别或信任边界，缺乏统一结构来分析风险在机器人系统中的来源、传播路径及干预点。作者旨在填补这一空白，为FM赋能的机器人系统提供系统化的S&P风险分析框架。

Method: 提出F-E-S-G四层结构边界框架：基础模型层(F)、具身系统层(E)、支撑生态系统层(S)、治理影响层(G)。在此基础上构建三级分类法：信任边界、安全隐私关注、风险缓解视角，并使用细粒度编码属性（目标、生命周期阶段、机制、系统访问、效果）对96篇论文进行标注和系统化分析。

Result: 识别出多种跨边界威胁模式、防御不匹配和评估缺口，这些从单一边界视角难以发现。

Conclusion: 基于分析结果提出了开放挑战和未来方向，为开发安全、隐私保护、负责任的FM驱动机器人系统提供研究路线图。

Abstract: Foundation models are reshaping robotics by enabling robots to interpret open-ended instructions, reason over multimodal contexts, and operate in complex, open-world environments. However, their integration also introduces security and privacy (S&P) risks that extend beyond the FMs themselves to embodied execution pipelines, supporting ecosystems, and broader governance impacts. Existing literature reviews provide valuable insights but often focus on specific FM types, risk categories, mitigation strategies, or trust boundaries. Consequently, the field lacks a unified structure for analyzing where risks originate, how they propagate across robotic systems, and where mitigations should intervene. To address this gap, we propose a progressive F-E-S-G structural boundary framework for analyzing the S&P of FM-powered robots. The framework comprises four layers: the Foundation model layer (F), Embodied system layer (E), Supporting ecosystem layer (S), and Governance impact layer (G). Building on this structure, we develop a multi-level taxonomy that organizes prior studies along three levels: F-E-S-G trust boundary, security-privacy concerns, and risk-mitigation perspectives. We further annotate each study using fine-grained coding attributes, including target, lifecycle stage, mechanism, system access, and effect. Guided by this framework and taxonomy, we systematize 96 papers. Our analysis uncovers multiple threat patterns, defense mismatches, and evaluation gaps that are difficult to identify from a single-boundary perspective. Based on these findings, we identify open challenges and future directions to provide a research agenda for developing secure, privacy-preserving, and responsibly governed FM-powered robotic systems.

</details>


### [338] [ATOM-Bench: A Real-World Benchmark for Atomic Skills and Compositional Generalization in Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16826)
*Zenan Wu,Bingqing Wei,Lu Liu,Zheqi He,Xi Wang,Jiakang Liu,Zehui Li,Guocai Yao,Jing-Shu Zheng,Xi Yang,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATOM-Bench是一个真实世界的操作策略基准，将桌面操作分解为30个原子任务和24个组合任务，通过2700次物理 rollout 评估发现当前策略在细粒度电机原子、计数和逻辑过滤上仍存在困难，且强原子性能不能可靠迁移到组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用操作策略虽然在演示任务上表现良好，但在细粒度原子技能的执行和技能组合泛化方面仍难以诊断，缺乏系统性的真实世界基准来区分失败源于弱电机执行、弱指令接地还是有限组合复用。

Method: 将桌面操作分解为电机原子和指令原子，构建30个原子任务和24个保留组合任务；收集3000个人类演示用于原子微调；提出Atomic Score (AS) 和 Compositional Failure Share (CFS) 指标来诊断故障来源；在五个代表性操纵策略上进行2700次物理 rollout 评估。

Result: 当前策略能获取简单指令接地技能，但细粒度电机原子、计数和逻辑过滤仍具挑战；强原子性能不能可靠迁移到保留组合任务。

Conclusion: ATOM-Bench为诊断操作策略中原子技能和组合泛化失败来源提供了系统的测试平台。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies are increasingly presented as foundation models for robotic control, but their real-world generalization remains difficult to diagnose. A policy may succeed on demonstrated tasks while still failing to execute fine-grained atomic skills or recombine learned skills in new task structures. We introduce \textbf{ATOM-Bench}, a real-world benchmark for evaluating both atomic skills and compositional generalization in manipulation policies. ATOM-Bench factorizes tabletop manipulation into motor atoms and instruction atoms, and contains 30 atomic tasks and 24 held-out compositional tasks across paired single-arm and dual-arm robot tracks. We collect 3,000 human demonstrations for atomic fine-tuning and release both the demonstration data and evaluation rollout data to support reproducible real-world evaluation. Policies are fine-tuned on atomic tasks and evaluated on both atomic skill acquisition and held-out compositional tasks. We further introduce Atomic Score (AS) and Compositional Failure Share (CFS) to distinguish failures caused by weak atomic skills from failures caused by limited compositional reuse. Through 2,700 physical rollouts on five representative manipulation policies, we find that current policies can acquire simple instruction-grounding skills, but still struggle with fine-grained motor atoms, counting, and logical filtering. More importantly, strong atomic performance does not reliably transfer to held-out compositional tasks. ATOM-Bench provides a diagnostic testbed for studying whether failures arise from weak motor execution, poor instruction grounding, or limited compositional reuse.

</details>


### [339] [Video-Based Optimal Transport for Feedback-Efficient Offline Preference-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16856)
*Tung M. Luu,Hwanhee Kim,Younghwan Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VOTP（Video-based Optimal Transport Preference）框架，利用视频基础模型（ViFMs）结合最优传输对齐视觉轨迹，从极少的人类偏好标签中生成高质量伪标签，实现半监督偏好强化学习，显著降低标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好强化学习（PbRL）虽然能从人类反馈中学习奖励函数，但标注成本高昂，限制了其可扩展性。现有方法需要大量人工偏好标签，亟需一种能利用少量标签高效学习奖励函数的方法。

Method: 利用预训练视频基础模型（ViFMs）的丰富表征空间，通过最优传输（Optimal Transport）对齐不同视频轨迹，从而为未标注数据生成高保真伪标签，实现半监督学习框架VOTP。

Result: 在 locomotion 和 manipulation 基准上超越现有离线 PbRL 方法；在有视觉干扰的环境下表现鲁棒；在真实机器人任务中仅需极少量人类输入即可学习有意义的奖励函数。

Conclusion: VOTP通过视频基础模型与最优传输的结合，有效解决了PbRL标注成本瓶颈，在有限标注预算下实现更优性能，且具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Conveying complex objectives to reinforcement learning (RL) agents often requires meticulous reward engineering. Preference-based RL (PbRL) offers a promising alternative by learning reward functions from human feedback, but its scalability is hindered by high labeling costs. Inspired by advances in Video Foundation Models (ViFMs), we present Video-based Optimal Transport Preference (VOTP), a semi-supervised framework that learns effective reward functions from only a handful of labels. By leveraging optimal transport to align visual trajectories within the rich representation space of ViFMs, VOTP effectively generates high-fidelity pseudo-labels for large amounts of unlabeled data, substantially reducing human supervision. Extensive experiments across locomotion and manipulation benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of VOTP, which outperforms state-of-the-art offline PbRL methods under limited feedback budgets. We also showcase the robustness of VOTP in the presence of visual distractors and validate its utility on real robotic tasks, where it learns meaningful rewards with minimal human input.

</details>


### [340] [ExoTraj: A General Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance Policy for Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16876)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Long Sun,Xu Liang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExoTraj，一种结合快速流匹配（flow matching）轨迹预测与模型预测控制（MPC）的外骨骼自适应辅助框架，无需昂贵动捕系统即可在复杂户外场景实现扭矩预测，跨被试预测误差降低14.0%，代谢率降低11.5-24.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态外骨骼场景中自适应扭矩预测依赖昂贵的动捕系统，在复杂户外环境中不可行；现有轨迹预测方法多为单步预测且忽视被试间差异，泛化性差；从轨迹到扭矩的映射因人-机器人系统高动态性和感知-控制强耦合而难以通过简单控制方法实现高效辅助。

Method: 1) 提出快速流匹配方法进行多步轨迹预测，利用轨迹生成误差和编码观测指导训练方向，提升预测精度和泛化性；2) 基于模型预测控制（MPC）设计新型优化目标优化扭矩，实现舒适鲁棒的辅助；3) 将轨迹预测与扭矩优化整合为统一策略ExoTraj。

Result: 在线阶段跨被试预测误差降低14.0%，对外部噪声具有鲁棒性；与零扭矩相比，代谢率降低11.5-24.4%，心率降低1.7-19.5%，峰值肌肉激活水平降低10.9-41.3%。

Conclusion: ExoTraj通过流匹配轨迹预测+MPC扭矩优化的统一框架，实现了无需高数据采集成本的户外自适应外骨骼辅助，在多个生理指标上取得显著改善。

Abstract: Adaptive torque prediction in dynamic exoskeleton scenarios requires expensive motion capture systems, which are infeasible in complex outdoor environments. Trajectory prediction has emerged as one of the effective approaches to address such an issue. However, the core challenges of exoskeleton trajectory prediction are twofold: establishing the mapping from multi-modal features to trajectory information; constructing the mapping from trajectory to torque. For the former, most existing methods perform only single-step prediction and neglect inter-subject trajectory variability, thereby limiting the trajectory optimization space and prediction generalization. To address this, this paper proposes a fast flow matching method that enables accurate trajectory prediction and better generalization for real-time performance, where trajectory generation errors and encoded observations are used to guide the training direction. For the second challenge, due to the high dynamics of the human-robot system and the strong coupling between perception and control, simple control methods struggle to achieve efficient assistance based on the predicted trajectory. This paper utilizes model predictive control and designs a novel optimization objective to optimize torque, ensuring the exoskeleton achieves comfortable and robust assistance. By integrating the above two components, the unified policy, denoted as ExoTraj, is developed to enable adaptive assistance in complex outdoor scenarios without high data acquisition cost. Experimental results show that compared to traditional methods, ExoTraj reduces cross-subject prediction error by 14.0% during the online phase and maintains robustness against external noise. Relative to the zero torque condition, ExoTraj decreases metabolic rate by 11.5-24.4%, heart rate by 1.7-19.5%, and peak muscle activation levels by 10.9-41.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [341] [SGM-SLAM: Scene Graph Matching for Data-Efficient Distributed SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16881)
*Yewei Huang,Tixiao Shan,Abhinav Rajvanshi,Niluthpol Chowdhury Mithun,Yaxuan Li,Brendan Englot,Han-Pang Chiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的分布式SLAM框架，利用场景图匹配实现多机器人间测量约束的识别，仅使用目标标签和质心而非特征级匹配，通过RGB-LiDAR融合点云构建语义点云层和离散物体层，采用多步数据交换与优化提升通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式SLAM依赖特征级匹配，通信开销大且对数据质量敏感，缺乏利用高层语义信息（如目标标签和质心）进行高效协作的方法。

Method: 融合RGB-LiDAR点云构建场景图（含语义点云层+离散有界物体层），通过交换物体标签和质心进行场景图匹配，采用多步数据交换与优化流程实现分布式SLAM。

Result: 在仿真和真实腿足机器人室内外数据集上验证了方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 首次仅用目标标签和质心进行场景图匹配的分布式SLAM，兼具数据高效和通信高效，适用于装备多传感器的机器人团队。

Abstract: We introduce a data-efficient distributed Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) framework designed for a team of robots equipped with LiDAR, cameras, and inertial sensors. Our framework uses scene graph matching to identify inter-robot measurement constraints. Unlike prior approaches that rely on feature-level matching, our framework is the first to perform scene graph matching using only object labels and centroids. Our approach constructs a scene graph by using fused RGB-LiDAR point clouds to generate both a semantically segmented point cloud layer, and a layer of discrete bounded objects, to accompany estimated robot trajectories. Scene graph matching is performed collaboratively through exchanging and matching object data with neighboring robots. To maximize communication efficiency, we utilize a multi-step data exchange and optimization process. We demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our approach using both simulation and real-world datasets collected by legged robots in indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [342] [LOPAL: Local Performance-Aware Active Learning from Imperfect Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16888)
*Johannes Heidersberger,Shail Jadav,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOPAL框架，利用局部演示质量信息的主动学习方法，通过高斯混合模型编码轨迹与局部质量评估，并结合共享自主机制主动收集修正数据，在不完美演示中提升机器人任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法忽略了演示质量在轨迹内部存在局部差异（演示中部分片段好、部分片段差），导致无法有效利用局部高质量信息来超越次优演示。

Method: 两个协同组件：1）局部性能驱动的LfD方法，用GMM同时编码演示轨迹及其局部质量评估，通过融合高表现局部数据生成超越原始演示的轨迹；2）主动数据采集，在缺乏优质数据的区域通过共享自主机制主动请求用户修正，机器人自主执行已学行为。

Result: 在真实管道检测任务中，任务性能提升高达27.31%，同时降低了演示采集所需的人力成本。

Conclusion: 利用局部演示质量信息并结合主动学习可以有效提升LfD在次优演示下的学习效果，降低人类示教负担。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables intuitive robot skill acquisition by allowing robots to learn directly from human task demonstrations. However, current methods often fail to address the fact that due to suboptimal and inconsistent human behavior, the quality of the demonstration can vary within each demonstration. Therefore, we introduce LOPAL (LOcal Performance-aware Active Learning), an active learning approach that leverages this local demonstration quality information. Our approach consists of two synergistic components. First, a local performance-driven LfD method uses a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) to encode both the demonstrated trajectories and their associated local quality assessments. This enables the generation of trajectories that outperform the imperfect demonstrations by utilizing complementary local data of high performance. Second, active data acquisition allows to improve beyond the imperfect demonstrations by collecting additional informative samples. In areas missing good data, the user is actively requested to provide corrections through a shared autonomy (SA) mechanism, while the robot autonomously executes the learned behavior. The efficacy of LOPAL was validated in both a simulation and a real-world experiment. The results from a real-world pipe inspection task showed that the proposed approach can achieve up to 27.31 % improvement in task performance while also reducing the effort required to collect the demonstrations.

</details>


### [343] [Binary Tracking for Spatial QA and Navigation with Open Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16902)
*Dongbin Na,Chanwoo Kim,Soonbin Rho,Giyun Choi,Gangbok Lee,Dooyoung Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BinTrack，一个全开源的基于二分搜索的时空定位智能体，用于服务机器人在长程自我中心路径上的空间问答，在SpaceLocQA全局类别上匹配GPT-4o水平，同时提升1.5倍推理速度，并发布GangnamLoop室外多行程基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间问答方法依赖闭源模型（如GPT-4o），但机器人在真实环境中因网络不稳定、延迟和部署成本无法可靠依赖在线闭源模型，而开源方法研究不足。

Method: 利用机器人轨迹的时间顺序，对查询中识别的两个锚点地标之间的轨迹段执行二分搜索定位，全开源实现。

Result: 准确率提升高达22.8%，在SpaceLocQA全局类别上匹配GPT-4o闭源模型结果，推理速度提升1.5倍以上，并发布GangnamLoop多行程室外基准。

Conclusion: 开源方法可替代闭源模型实现同等性能的空间问答，适合机器人机载部署，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This work addresses spatial question answering for service robots traversing long egocentric routes. Given a query such as "where can I find a dry cleaner on the way back home?", the system returns a metric coordinate that downstream navigation components can act on. Prior Spatial Question Answering approaches leverage retrieval-augmented agents built on closed-source models such as GPT-4o for path exploration. However, robots operating in the real world often cannot reliably depend on online closed-source models due to network instability, communication latency, and deployment cost. It creates a need for open-source based Spatial Question Answering approaches that can run onboard the robot, yet prior research in this direction remains limited. This work proposes BinTrack, a simple yet effective, fully open-source spatial-localization agent that leverages the temporal ordering of a robot's trajectory. BinTrack performs a binary search over the trajectory segments between two anchor landmarks identified from a query. It improves overall accuracy by up to 22.8% over other open-source implementations and even matches the reported closed-source model result on the global category of the SpaceLocQA benchmark, the most challenging setting that has so far required strong reasoning agents such as GPT-4o. Furthermore, its optimized inference strategy consistently yields more than a 1.5x inference speedup over previous approaches. Finally, this work releases GangnamLoop, a novel and practical multi-trip outdoor benchmark collected by deploying a real quadruped robot on public streets with the anonymization policy. It revisits the same locations under different outdoor conditions and pairs the robot's low viewpoint with the human owner's. The source codes and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/ndb796/BinaryTracking

</details>


### [344] [Unified Motion-Action Modeling for Heterogeneous Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16917)
*Yunhao Cao,Shitong Liu,Chao Feng,Meryl Zhang,Xuanchen Lu,Andrew Owens,Kuan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMA模型以3D物体运动轨迹作为共享接口，通过掩码生成目标统一视觉运动控制和动力学建模，支持多任务预训练并在多种推理模式下超越专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将视觉运动控制和动力学建模分离处理，缺乏统一的接口和框架来利用异构数据（机器人演示、人类视频、模拟数据）进行联合预训练。

Method: 以3D物体运动轨迹作为共享接口，采用掩码生成目标联合建模物体运动与机器人动作；使用事后重标注的运动上下文和对比学习解耦任务意图与场景几何；同一预训练参数支持运动条件控制、动力学建模和少样本任务适应。

Result: 在机器人演示、人类视频和模拟数据混合预训练后，在三种推理模式下均一致超越现有专用SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 3D运动轨迹可以作为连接视觉运动控制与动力学建模的有效共享接口，掩码生成预训练框架能充分利用异构数据源。

Abstract: We present Unified Motion-Action (UMA) Model, an approach that uses 3D object motion trajectories as a shared interface to bridge visuomotor control and dynamics modeling. UMA treats object motion and robot actions as co-evolving variables under a masked generative objective, in which the mask pattern determines both the supervision regime during pretraining and the inference mode at deployment. Using hindsight-relabeled motion contexts and a contrastive objective that disentangles task intent from scene geometry, UMA enables multi-task pretraining across heterogeneous data sources without requiring manually annotated task instructions. At deployment, the same pretrained parameters support motion-conditioned visuomotor control, motion-based dynamics modeling, and task adaptation from few-shot demonstrations. Pretrained on a mixture of robot demonstrations, human videos, and simulated data, UMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines specialized for each inference mode.

</details>


### [345] [CrossMaps: Confidence-Aware Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping for Rover Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16935)
*Jan-Niklas Klein,Sona Ghahremani,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrossMaps 是一个实时、置信度感知的开放词汇语义建图管线，通过多尺度 CLIP 嵌入、置信度融合和双记忆架构（STM+LTM），在 Jetson Orin 驱动的 UGV 上实现 RGB-D 数据的自然语言查询语义地图，以支持导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图方法（如 VLMaps）缺乏对感知置信度（如测距可靠性、光照伪影、数据密度）的显式建模，难以在部分可观测环境下指导数据融合和导航决策。CrossMaps 旨在耦合感知与导航过程，构建置信度感知的语义地图。

Method: 基于 VLMaps 风格，集成多尺度 CLIP 嵌入 + 置信度感知融合 + 双记忆架构（STM 聚合噪声观测，LTM 存储高置信持久语义地标），部署于 Jetson Orin + SLAM 的 UGV 上。

Result: 实时运行，生成可用自然语言查询的语义热力图以指导导航。

Conclusion: 通过置信度感知的融合和双记忆机制，提升了语义地图在部分可观测条件下的鲁棒性和可用性，为感知-导航耦合研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Rovers rely on perception to maintain spatial maps that encode both objects and sensor quality (e.g., range reliability, lighting artifacts, data density), guiding data fusion, embedding updates, and navigation under partial observability. To study these coupled perception-navigation processes, we present CrossMaps, a real-time confidence-aware open-vocabulary semantic mapping pipeline that constructs language-queryable maps from RGB-D data. Building on VLMaps-style approaches, CrossMaps integrates multi-scale CLIP embeddings with confidence-aware fusion and a dual-memory architecture consisting of Short-Term Memory (STM) and Long-Term Memory (LTM). The STM aggregates noisy visual observations using geometric, semantic, and temporal confidence cues, while confident and coherent cells are promoted to the LTM as persistent semantic landmarks. Designed for deployment with a Jetson Orin-powered UGV alongside SLAM, CrossMaps runs in real time and produces semantic heatmaps that can be queried with natural language to guide rover navigation.

</details>


### [346] [SidewalkBench: Benchmarking Visual Navigation on Urban Sidewalks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16953)
*Zhizheng Liu,Honglin He,Vivek Alumootil,Akshat Pandya,Brad Squicciarini,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SidewalkBench是首个面向城市人行道视觉导航的统一基准，基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建，包含330个单元测试、800个行人反应场景和105个长程场景，评估了9个视觉导航模型，发现行人交互和长程鲁棒性是现有模型的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航模型缺乏统一的人行道导航基准，导致评估不可复现且缺乏标准化，无法有效量化模型在复杂结构布局、动态行人行为和长距离导航中的表现。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建GPU加速的高保真人行道仿真环境，包含程序化生成和真实扫描场景，并引入反应式事件驱动的行人行为和灵活高效的动画，构建330个单元测试、800个行人反应场景和105个长程场景来标准化评估。

Result: 评估了9个视觉导航模型，发现行人交互和长程鲁棒性是现有模型的关键瓶颈，使用合成数据扩展人行道训练是有效的解决方案。

Conclusion: SidewalkBench为城市人行道视觉导航提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了现有模型在行人交互和长程导航方面的不足，并为使用合成数据训练提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Urban sidewalk navigation presents significant challenges due to complex structural layouts, dynamic pedestrian behaviors, and long distances. While recent visual navigation models offer a promising solution, the lack of a unified benchmark hinders quantitative and reproducible evaluation. To bridge this gap, we propose SidewalkBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed for visual navigation on urban sidewalks. Built upon NVIDIA Isaac Sim, SidewalkBench brings GPU-accelerated simulation of diverse, high-fidelity sidewalk environments, including both procedurally generated and real-world scanned scenes. We further populate the scenes with rich, reactive event-based pedestrian behaviors and flexible, efficient animation, enabling standardized model evaluation under realistic real-world settings. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 9 visual navigation models on 330 unit-test scenarios, 800 pedestrian-reactive scenarios, and 105 long-horizon scenarios. Our findings highlight that pedestrian interaction and long-horizon robustness remain critical bottlenecks for existing models, and scaling up sidewalk training with synthetic data emerges as a promising solution.

</details>


### [347] [When Should a Robot Replan? Regret-Guided Update Scheduling in Time-Varying MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16972)
*Negin Musavi,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Ruben Hernandez,William Schafer,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人在非平稳环境中因预算限制无法频繁重规划的问题，提出了一种skip-update方案，在选定的更新时间点进行状态估计和策略计算，间隔期内复用策略，并基于动态遗憾分析给出自适应更新规则，在火星车导航和Crazyflie四旋翼飞行中优于其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非平稳环境（如动力学漂移）中需要持续适应，但机载能量和计算预算限制了全状态估计和重规划的频率，因此需要研究何时应该花费有限的预算进行更新。

Method: 将问题建模为具有转移漂移率已知界限的时变MDP，提出skip-update方案：在选定的更新时间点用最大似然估计转移核并计算有限时域策略，间隔期内复用策略并传播状态估计。通过动态遗憾分析导出遗憾增长界限，从而得到自适应更新规则。

Result: 在模拟火星车导航（时变滑移动力学）和真实Crazyflie四旋翼（室内障碍物场）中，自适应分配方法优于其他预算受限基线。

Conclusion: 提出的遗憾引导的自适应更新规则能够有效分配有限预算，在非平稳环境中的机器人导航任务上表现更优。

Abstract: Robots operating in non-stationary environments must continually adapt their policies as the dynamics drift, but onboard energy and compute budgets cap how often a full state estimation and re-planning step can be performed. This raises a question: \emph{when}, along a horizon, should a robot spend its limited budget? We formulate this problem in time-varying Markov decision processes (TVMDPs) with a known bound on the rate of transition drift. We model execution as a \emph{skip-update} scheme in which, at chosen update times, the agent estimates the transition kernel by maximum likelihood and computes a finite-horizon policy, and between updates reuses this policy under a propagated state estimate. We analyze the dynamic regret of this scheme and show how it grows during skip intervals in terms of the properties of the TVMDP and the skip lengths; the resulting bound answers the opening question via an online, regret-guided update rule that allocates the budget adaptively. We evaluate the rule in a simulated Mars-rover navigation task with time-varying slip dynamics and on a Crazyflie quadrotor in indoor obstacle fields. Adaptive allocation outperforms other budgeted baselines.

</details>


### [348] [Task-Error Residual Learning for Real-Robot Five-Ball Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16978)
*Kai Ploeger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于残差学习（residual learning）的方法，利用方向性任务误差（directional task-error）监督和任务误差模型驱动的样本选择，在Barrett WAM机械臂上实现了三、四、五球的稳定抛接球。系统从第二次尝试开始收敛（首次掉落），任务误差单调递减无后续失败。与传统强化学习的标量奖励相比，方向性反馈和信息利用效率是关键瓶颈，而非底层规划控制栈的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在残差学习场景中样本效率低，原因是标量奖励信息量不足，且随机探索进一步浪费反馈信息。本文旨在通过方向性任务误差监督和样本选择机制提升残差学习效率，验证信息内容与信息利用是两个独立且都必要的维度。

Method: 1) 使用方向性任务误差（directional task-error）替代标量奖励作为监督信号；2) 构建任务误差模型驱动样本选择；3) 在三个二元轴上比较：学习反馈的方向性信息（directional information）、分析先验的承诺度（commitment），涵盖Newton-style Jacobian更新、Composite Bayesian Optimization、随机搜索方法；4) 最终采用固定Jacobian的Newton更新作为最可靠方案。

Result: 在Barrett WAM机械臂上实现三、四、五球抛接；首次尝试失败后，第二次尝试开始收敛；任务误差单调递减无后续失败；残差可容忍先验偏差和降级关节跟踪；方向性反馈和信息利用两者都必要，缺一不可；最简单的固定Jacobian Newton更新最可靠。

Conclusion: 残差学习在真实机器人上的瓶颈是监督信号的信息含量和学习者的信息利用方式，而非底层规划控制栈的精度。方向性反馈和充分信息利用是两个正交且都必要的维度。

Abstract: For residual learning that refines existing behavior, sample efficiency depends on two things: how much information each rollout returns, and how efficiently the learner uses that information. Reinforcement learning's standard scalar reward carries far less information than the directional task error that defines the task. Random exploration further discards whatever information each rollout returns. Through residual learning with directional task-error supervision and a task error model that drives sample selection, we achieve stable three-, four-, and five-ball juggling on anthropomorphic Barrett WAM arms. Despite planning and controlling through a simple, idealized stack, the system converges from the second attempt. The first attempt drops, after which task error decreases monotonically without further failures. In comparison, five-ball juggling typically takes humans years of practice. We compare residual learners across two ternary axes, the directional information in the learning feedback and the commitment of the analytic prior, spanning Newton-style Jacobian updates, Composite Bayesian Optimization, and stochastic search methods. Both axes prove necessary: neither directional feedback nor an informative prior suffices alone, and the simplest method that combines them, a fixed-Jacobian Newton update, is the most reliable. The learned residual tolerates substantial prior misalignment and degraded joint tracking, affecting mainly convergence speed. The bottleneck for residual learning on real robots is therefore the information content of the supervision signal and how the learner uses it, not the accuracy of the surrounding stack. Video documentation of all experiments is available at https://kai-ploeger.com/residual-juggling.

</details>


### [349] [ROVE: Unlocking Human Interventions for Humanoid Manipulation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17011)
*Wei Xiao,Weiliang Tang,Yuying Ge,Hui Zhou,Yao Mu,Li Zhang,Yixiao Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROVE框架，通过强化学习结合乐观值估计（OVE）和跨本体人类经验视频，在非完美人工干预数据上训练人形VLA模型，解决干预轨迹次优问题，在真实世界人形操作任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形VLA模型的后训练依赖人类干预提供修正信号，但由于人形机器人全身运动学和灵巧手控制的复杂性，干预轨迹常是次优的（犹豫、低效甚至错误），直接模仿这些轨迹会吸收不良行为。现有方法无法同时解决系统层面（难以采集高质量干预数据）和算法层面（如何从混合质量数据中学习）的挑战。

Method: 1) 人机协同管线：采集部署轨迹和人类干预轨迹；2) 乐观值估计（OVE）：从混合质量轨迹中优先选择高价值行为；3) 跨本体人类经验视频：利用人类操作视频作为监督信号，增强对长尾失败和恢复模式的价值估计鲁棒性；4) Critic产生信息性优势信号，引导VLA actor关注高价值行为而非盲目模仿所有动作。

Result: 在真实世界接触密集和精细化的人形操作任务上，ROVE优于经验学习基线，并在多轮干预-部署迭代中持续改善表现。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习与乐观值估计结合跨本体人类视频先验，可以有效从非完美人类干预中学习，提升人形VLA模型的后训练效果。

Abstract: Human interventions provide crucial corrective signals for post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, enabling seamless humanoid interventions is a formidable systems challenge due to complex whole-body kinematics and dexterous-hand control. Consequently, the collected intervention trajectories are often suboptimal, and methods that rely on human interventions as expert supervision can absorb hesitant, inefficient, or even erroneous behaviors. To address both the system and algorithmic challenges, we propose ROVE, a reinforcement learning framework for humanoid VLA post-training with imperfect human interventions. First, ROVE introduces a human-in-the-loop pipeline capable of collecting deployment and intervention data for humanoid manipulation. Second, it utilizes Optimistic Value Estimation (OVE) to prioritize high-value behaviors from mixed-quality trajectories. To further robustify value estimation, we incorporate cross-embodiment human experience videos to provide rich supervision for long-tailed failure and recovery modes. The resulting critic yields informative advantage signals, steering the VLA actor to focus on high-value behaviors rather than indiscriminately imitating all actions. On challenging real-world contact-rich and fine-grained humanoid manipulation tasks, ROVE outperforms experience-learning baselines and consistently improves across multiple rollout-intervention iterations.

</details>


### [350] [R2RDreamer: 3D-aware Data Augmentation for Spatially-generalized 2D Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17040)
*Xiuwei Xu,Haowen Sun,Angyuan Ma,Yiwei Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Bingyao Yu,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2RDreamer是一种实到实的数据增强框架，通过3D轻量级编辑（点云+轨迹）结合2D视频补全（遮挡感知投影+密集控制图像到视频模型）来增强模仿学习策略的空间泛化能力，在多种策略上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习的操作策略在空间泛化上严重依赖大规模多样化数据采集，而现有数据增强方法要么受限于仿真到真实的差距，要么依赖强3D场景解析且输出仅适配3D点云策略，缺乏既保持几何一致性又支持2D RGB策略的轻量级实到实增强方法。

Method: 1) 轻量级3D增强：在共享3D坐标系中编辑不完整物体点云和末端执行器轨迹；2) 遮挡感知投影：将编辑后的3D场景投影到带遮挡推理的掩码图像空间控制视频；3) 视频补全：使用密集控制图像到视频模型完成时序连贯的RGB观测。

Result: 在空间偏移的操作任务上，R2RDreamer同时提升了2D扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作策略的泛化性能，消融实验验证了3D编辑、遮挡感知投影和视频补全各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: R2RDreamer通过3D编辑+2D视频补全的混合范式有效提升了空间泛化能力，弥合了3D几何一致性与2D RGB策略兼容性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Spatial generalization is critical for imitation-learned manipulation policies, but achieving it typically requires scaling demonstrations across diverse object poses, robot configurations, and camera viewpoints. Data augmentation from a few source demonstrations offers a practical alternative to costly real-world collection. Simulation-based augmentation can create controllable variation, but requires complex environment and object setup and may introduce a sim-to-real gap. Recent real-to-real methods avoid these issues by jointly editing 3D observations and action trajectories from real demonstrations, yet they still rely on strong 3D scene parsing and geometry completion, and often produce observations tailored to 3D pointcloud policies rather than RGB-based 2D policies. We propose R2RDreamer, a real-to-real demonstration augmentation framework that preserves the geometric consistency of 3D action-observation editing while moving visual completion to 2D video space. Specifically, R2RDreamer first performs lightweight 3D augmentation by editing incomplete object pointclouds and end-effector trajectories in a shared 3D frame; it then projects the edited scene into masked image-space control videos with occlusion-aware reasoning and uses a dense-control image-to-video model to complete temporally coherent RGB observations. Experiments on spatially shifted manipulation tasks with both 2D diffusion-style policies and vision-language-action policies show that R2RDreamer improves spatial generalization from limited source demonstrations, with analyses validating the contributions of 3D editing, occlusion-aware projection, and video completion.

</details>


### [351] [Hierarchical Advantage Weighting for Online RL Fine-Tuning of VLAs from Sparse Episode Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17043)
*Tongyan Fang,Siyuan Huang,Naiyu Fang,Ganlong Zhao,Zhongjin Luo,Jianbo Liu,Xiaogang Wang,Ying Dong,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA策略在线RL微调中稀疏二值奖励无法区分可行性与效率、且混入干预片段导致错误信用分配的问题，提出HABC框架：分别为可行性和效率训练独立Critic头，通过状态自适应门控合并优势信号，并引入干预感知的信用分配机制。在三类双臂接触式任务中，将成功率从SFT基线的12-44%提升至38-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA在线RL微调中，每轮 rollout 只产生单一成功/失败标签，转化为标量奖励会混淆"可行性"与"效率"两个目标；同时真实场景中自主与干预片段混合，粗暴地将回合结果分配给所有转移会导致错误信用分配。需要一种能解耦两个目标、且正确处理干预边界的信用分配方法。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC)：1) 为可行性(viability)和效率(efficiency)训练两个独立的Critic头，分别在不同数据子集上训练；2) 状态自适应门控$g_t$合并两个单步优势，在成功不确定时优先可行性，可行性高时转向效率；3) 将合并优势转换为Actor损失的每转移权重；4) 干预感知信用分配：仅将结果标签限制在当前策略执行的片段，防止跨干预边界的监督泄漏。

Result: 在三个真实双臂接触式操作任务上，HABC将SFT基线成功率从36%/44%/12%提升至92%/88%/38%。

Conclusion: HABC通过解耦可行性与效率两个目标并引入干预感知信用分配，显著提升了VLA策略在真实机器人上的在线RL微调效果，尤其在接触式双臂任务中表现突出。

Abstract: When pretrained VLA policies are fine-tuned through online RL, each rollout episode produces only a single binary outcome (success or failure), yet the actor update requires per-transition supervision. Existing approaches commonly reduce this sparse outcome to a single scalar reward or advantage signal, which conflates distinct forms of transition-level feedback and provides limited guidance once basic task success becomes achievable. First, a single scalar signal conflates the two objectives of viability and efficiency; once basic success is achieved, the binary label provides no gradient to distinguish efficient completions from slow ones. Second, real-world rollouts mix autonomous and intervention segments; naively assigning episode outcomes across these boundaries introduces incorrect credit assignment. To address these issues, we propose Hierarchical Advantage-Weighted Behavior Cloning (HABC), which trains separate critic heads for these two objectives on different data subsets and combines their outputs with a state-adaptive balance. A state-adaptive gate $g_t$ merges their one-step advantages, prioritizing viability when success is uncertain and shifting to efficiency only when viability is high, and converts the result into per-transition weights on the actor loss. Intervention-aware credit assignment further restricts outcome labels to segments executed by the current policy, preventing supervision from leaking across intervention boundaries. In real-robot experiments on three contact-rich bimanual tasks, HABC raises success from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines of 36%, 44%, and 12% to 92%, 88%, and 38%.

</details>


### [352] [Geometric Action Model for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17046)
*Jisang Han,Seonghu Jeon,Jaewoo Jung,René Zurbrügg,Honggyu An,Tifanny Portela,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Seungryong Kim,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAM提出将预训练的几何基础模型（GFM）拆分为浅层编码器+因果未来预测器+深层解码器的架构，用单一骨干同时完成几何感知、语言条件时序预测和动作解码，在仿真和真实机器人操作任务上超越现有VLA/WAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和WAM主要基于2D图像或2D衍生潜空间操作，缺乏接触丰富的操作任务所需的显式3D几何先验，导致物理交互准确性不足。

Method: 将GFM在中间层拆分：浅层作为观测编码器，中间插入因果未来预测器（用语言、本体感受和动作历史条件预测未来潜变量），深层块继续传播特征并解码出未来几何和动作，最小化架构修改下赋予GFM语言条件时序世界建模能力。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实机器人操作基准上，GAM在精度、鲁棒性、速度和模型轻量化方面均超越当前基于基础模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过复用几何基础模型作为感知、时序预测和动作解码的统一基底，可以在保持强几何先验的同时高效实现语言条件的世界模型与操作策略，是VLA/WAM的一个有前景的新方向。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies must follow user instructions while reasoning about how objects, cameras, and robot actions interact in the 3D physical world. Recent vision-language-action models (VLAs) and video world-action models (WAMs) inherit strong semantic or temporal priors from large-scale foundation models, but they still operate primarily on 2D image frames or 2D-derived latent spaces, leaving implicit the 3D geometry required for contact-rich manipulation. We propose the Geometric Action Model (GAM), a language-conditioned manipulation policy that directly repurposes a pretrained geometric foundation model (GFM) as a shared substrate for perception, temporal prediction, and action decoding. GAM splits the GFM at an intermediate layer: the shallow layers serve as an observation encoder, and a causal future predictor inserted at the split layer forecasts future latent tokens conditioned on language, proprioception, and action history. The predicted future tokens are then routed through the remaining GFM blocks for feature propagation and decoding, allowing a single backbone to produce both future geometry and actions. This design equips the GFM with language-conditioned temporal world modeling through minimal architectural modification while preserving its rich geometric priors. Across a broad suite of simulation and real-robot manipulation benchmarks, GAM is more accurate, more robust, faster, and lighter than current foundation-model-scale baselines.

</details>


### [353] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HUG模型，基于流匹配从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化人类抓取姿态，借助智能眼镜采集100万帧第一人称抓取数据集1M-HUGs（6,707个物体实例），并构建HUG-Bench仿真基准，在90个未见物体上评估，真实世界30物体测试集上比SOTA提升23-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以轻松抓取各种物体，但多指机器人远未达到这一水平。现有机器人抓取方法缺乏对人类自然抓取多样性的建模，而人类每天抓取数千物体的行为是最自然的数据来源。

Method: 使用智能眼镜收集第一人称RGB-D抓取数据集1M-HUGs；提出基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成模型，融合RGB和深度观测，输出参数化的手腕平移、旋转和MANO手部姿态；预测的抓取可通过重定位映射到多种机器人手部。

Result: 在HUG-Bench的30个真实世界物体测试集上，比SOTA抓取基线提升23%和34%。数据集覆盖41栋建筑、6,707个物体实例、27.8小时数据。

Conclusion: 人类抓取数据可作为机器人抓取的自然来源，流匹配模型能有效生成多样化自然抓取姿态，并可零样本迁移到多种机器人手部，在真实场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


### [354] [T-Rex: Tactile-Reactive Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17055)
*Dantong Niu,Zhuoyang Liu,Zekai Wang,Boning Shao,Zhao-Heng Yin,Anirudh Pai,Yuvan Sharma,Stefano Saravalle,Ruijie Zheng,Jing Wang,Ryan Punamiya,Mengda Xu,Yuqi Xie,Yunfan Jiang,Letian Fu,Konstantinos Kallidromitis,Matteo Gioia,Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Haiwen Feng,Fabio Galasso,Wei Zhan,David M. Chan,Yutong Bai,Roei Herzig,Jiahui Lei,Fei-Fei Li,Ken Goldberg,Jitendra Malik,Pieter Abbeel,Yuke Zhu,Danfei Xu,Jim,Fan,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模触觉数据集（100小时）和变速率混合Transformer（MoT）架构，结合时序触觉VQ-VAE编码器，使VLA模型具备触觉反应能力，在12个精细操作任务上平均成功率比最强基线高30%+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在机器人操作中普遍忽略触觉模态，或仅使用静态触觉编码器，原因包括缺乏大规模训练数据、标准化评估不足、架构限制以及静态编码器的局限。本文旨在突破这些限制，实现触觉反应式灵巧操作。

Method: 1) 收集100小时大规模触觉丰富数据集，采用优先基础运动基元的数据高效采集方案；2) 提出变速率混合Transformer（MoT）架构，配备新型时序触觉VQ-VAE编码器，以高效利用高频触觉信号而不牺牲VLA现有能力。

Result: 在12个精细力控制和可变形物体操作任务上，触觉反应式策略平均成功率比最强基线高出30%以上。

Conclusion: 触觉模态对灵巧操作至关重要，本文通过大规模数据集和新型MoT架构有效解决了触觉反应式策略的瓶颈，显著提升了复杂操作任务的性能。

Abstract: The ability to react dynamically to tactile signals has long been considered crucial to agile human-level dexterity. Yet contemporary learning-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for robotic manipulation generally either overlook the tactile modality or are limited to encoders with static cues, due in part to the scarcity of diverse training data and standardized evaluation, architectural constraints in current VLA models, and limitations of static tactile encoders. In this paper, we push the frontier of tactile-reactive manipulation by addressing all of these limitations. We propose a large-scale, 100-hour tactile-rich dataset collected via a novel, data-efficient recipe that prioritizes elementary motor primitives. To effectively exploit naturally high-frequency touch signals without sacrificing the existing capabilities of existing VLAs, we introduce a variable-rate Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture equipped with a novel temporal tactile VQ-VAE encoder. We demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-reactive policies on 12 manipulation tasks requiring delicate force control and deformable object manipulation, achieving over 30% higher average success rate than the strongest baseline.
